{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01StNuJsstvmvkewXefv4ZAa","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→```md\n     2→# SynestheticCanvas – Architectural Overview\n     3→> Document Path: `SynestheticCanvas/docs/architecture/overview.md`  \n     4→> Project: **SynestheticCanvas API Suite (`api_graphql`)**  \n     5→> Revision: `v1.2.3` – _last updated 2024-06-14_\n     6→\n     7→---\n     8→\n     9→## 1. Vision\n    10→\n    11→SynestheticCanvas transforms every HTTP or WebSocket request into a _brush-stroke_ on a living digital canvas.  \n    12→Microservices supply the colors, textures, and motion, while the **GraphQL/REST gateway** orchestrates them into a single, time-synchronous response.  \n    13→The architecture has three non-negotiable goals:\n    14→\n    15→1. **Expressiveness** – Artists can combine services like mixing paint.  \n    16→2. **Evolution** – New art forms (e.g., volumetric audio) can be added without breaking existing exhibitions.  \n    17→3. **Performance** – Frame-accurate (< 16 ms) responses even under festival-scale load.\n    18→\n    19→---\n    20→\n    21→## 2. Macro-Level Topology\n    22→\n    23→```mermaid\n    24→flowchart LR\n    25→    subgraph Client Edge\n    26→        VR[VR Headset]\n    27→        Web[Web Frontend]\n    28→        Bot[AI Bot]\n    29→    end\n    30→\n    31→    subgraph Gateway Layer\n    32→        GQL(GraphQL Gateway)\n    33→        REST(REST Fallback)\n    34→    end\n    35→\n    36→    subgraph Core Services\n    37→        Palette(Palette Svc)\n    38→        Synth(Texture Synth)\n    39→        Audio(Audio-React)\n    40→        Narr(Narrative Engine)\n    41→    end\n    42→    \n    43→    subgraph Shared Infra\n    44→        LogStack(ELK/OTel)\n    45→        Redis((Redis Cache))\n    46→        MQ((NATS JetStream))\n    47→        PG[(PostgreSQL Cluster)]\n    48→    end\n    49→\n    50→    VR -->|Query / Mutation| GQL\n    51→    Web --> GQL\n    52→    Bot --> REST\n    53→    \n    54→    GQL -- gRPC/json --> Palette & Synth & Audio & Narr\n    55→    REST -- HTTP--> Palette & Synth & Audio & Narr\n    56→    \n    57→    GQL -- sub/ms logs --> LogStack\n    58→    REST -- prometheus --> LogStack\n    59→    \n    60→    Palette --- PG\n    61→    Synth --- Redis\n    62→    Audio --- MQ\n    63→    Narr  --- PG\n    64→\n    65→    classDef red fill:#ffdfe0,stroke:#ce2b37,stroke-width:2;\n    66→    class REST red\n    67→```\n    68→\n    69→---\n    70→\n    71→## 3. Layered Breakdown\n    72→\n    73→| Layer                | Responsibility                                           | Key Patterns                        |\n    74→|----------------------|----------------------------------------------------------|-------------------------------------|\n    75→| **Gateway**          | Auth, rate-limit, schema stitching, cache-aside          | API Gateway, GraphQL Schema         |\n    76→| **Service Layer**    | Domain orchestrations, streaming, CQRS                   | Command/Query Separation            |\n    77→| **Repository Layer** | Data access, DAO mapping, migrations                     | Repository Pattern                  |\n    78→| **Infra**            | Observability, tracing, message bus, persistence         | Sidecar + 12-Factor                 |\n    79→\n    80→---\n    81→\n    82→## 4. Gateway Internals\n    83→\n    84→### 4.1 GraphQL Route Lifecycle\n    85→\n    86→```mermaid\n    87→sequenceDiagram\n    88→    participant C as Client\n    89→    participant G as Gateway\n    90→    participant V as Validator\n    91→    participant S as Service\n    92→    participant L as Logger\n    93→    \n    94→    C->>G: gql { mutateTexture(...) }\n    95→    G->>V: schema.validate(request)\n    96→    V->>G: Ok / Err\n    97→    alt Err\n    98→        G->>C: 422 Unprocessable Entity\n    99→        G->>L: warn(validation)\n   100→    else Ok\n   101→        G->>S: gRPC call (texture-synth)\n   102→        S-->>G: TexturePayload\n   103→        G->>L: info(request)\n   104→        G->>C: 200 + JSON\n   105→    end\n   106→```\n   107→\n   108→#### 4.1.1 Validation Rules\n   109→* **Static** – schema-compiled (enum ranges, non-null fields)  \n   110→* **Dynamic** – cross-service (`audioId` exists, `paletteId` owns user)  \n   111→* **Security** – depth limit, cost analysis, JWT claims scoping  \n   112→\n   113→### 4.2 REST Fallback\n   114→\n   115→* Code-generated from the GraphQL schema using `gnostic` + custom templates.  \n   116→* Follows HATEOAS, supports `?page[limit]=`, `?page[offset]=` for pagination.  \n   117→\n   118→---\n   119→\n   120→## 5. Versioning Strategy\n   121→\n   122→1. Breaking changes → new _major_ (e.g., `v2`) schema namespace.  \n   123→2. Additive changes → patch bump (`v1.3 → v1.4`) and feature flag.  \n   124→3. Deprecated fields emit `Deprecation-Notice` headers for 90 days.  \n   125→\n   126→```\n   127→query v1_3 {\n   128→  palette(id: \"ultra-violet\") {          # valid\n   129→    swatches { hex }                     \n   130→  }\n   131→}\n   132→\n   133→query v2_0 {\n   134→  paletteGet(id: \"ultra-violet\") {       # renamed\n   135→    swatches { hex, rgb }                # new field\n   136→  }\n   137→}\n   138→```\n   139→\n   140→---\n   141→\n   142→## 6. Service Blueprint (C Reference)\n   143→\n   144→A microservice is a standalone **statically-linked** ELF built with **C17** and a thin **gRPC** layer.  \n   145→Below is a simplified excerpt from `palette_svc/src/service.c`.\n   146→\n   147→```c\n   148→/**\n   149→ * @file service.c\n   150→ * @brief Palette microservice – Service entry point & request handlers.\n   151→ */\n   152→\n   153→#include <stdlib.h>\n   154→#include <signal.h>\n   155→#include \"core/palette.h\"\n   156→#include \"infra/logger.h\"\n   157→#include \"infra/repository.h\"\n   158→#include \"gen/palette.grpc.pb-c.h\"\n   159→\n   160→static palette_repo_t *repo = NULL;\n   161→\n   162→/* gRPC → C callback ------------------------------------------------------- */\n   163→static void handle_palette_get(\n   164→        const PaletteGetRequest *req,\n   165→        PaletteGetReply **resp,\n   166→        void *user_data)\n   167→{\n   168→    (void)user_data;\n   169→    sc_trace_span_t span = sc_trace_start(\"palette_get\");\n   170→\n   171→    /* 1. Validate -------------------------------- */\n   172→    if (!req->id || req->id[0] == '\\0') {\n   173→        sc_log_warn(\"palette_get: empty id\");\n   174→        *resp = palette_get_reply__error(\n   175→            HTTP_BAD_REQUEST, \"Palette ID must not be empty\");\n   176→        goto finish;\n   177→    }\n   178→\n   179→    /* 2. Repository ------------------------------ */\n   180→    palette_t pal = {0};\n   181→    sc_error_t err = repo->get(repo, req->id, &pal);\n   182→    if (err.code == SC_ERR_NOT_FOUND) {\n   183→        *resp = palette_get_reply__error(\n   184→            HTTP_NOT_FOUND, \"Palette not found\");\n   185→        goto finish;\n   186→    } else if (err.code != SC_OK) {\n   187→        sc_log_error(\"repo->get failed: %s\", err.msg);\n   188→        *resp = palette_get_reply__error(\n   189→            HTTP_INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR, \"Internal error\");\n   190→        goto finish;\n   191→    }\n   192→\n   193→    /* 3. Mapping → ProtoBuf ---------------------- */\n   194→    *resp = palette_get_reply__success(&pal);\n   195→\n   196→finish:\n   197→    sc_trace_end(&span);\n   198→}\n   199→\n   200→int main(int argc, char **argv)\n   201→{\n   202→    sc_logger_init_default();          /* JSON-structured logs          */\n   203→    sc_trace_init_otel();              /* OpenTelemetry exporter        */\n   204→\n   205→    repo = palette_repo_init(getenv(\"PG_DSN\"));\n   206→    if (!repo) {\n   207→        sc_log_fatal(\"failed to init repository\");\n   208→        return EXIT_FAILURE;\n   209→    }\n   210→\n   211→    sc_grpc_server_t *srv = sc_grpc_server_create(\"0.0.0.0:7001\");\n   212→    sc_grpc_server_register_palette_get(srv, handle_palette_get, NULL);\n   213→\n   214→    /* Graceful shutdown ------------------------- */\n   215→    signal(SIGTERM, sc_grpc_server_stop);\n   216→    signal(SIGINT,  sc_grpc_server_stop);\n   217→\n   218→    sc_log_info(\"palette_svc listening on :7001\");\n   219→    sc_grpc_server_serve(srv);\n   220→\n   221→    palette_repo_free(repo);\n   222→    sc_grpc_server_free(srv);\n   223→    sc_logger_flush();\n   224→    return EXIT_SUCCESS;\n   225→}\n   226→```\n   227→\n   228→---\n   229→\n   230→## 7. Observability & Monitoring\n   231→\n   232→| Metric                    | Target             | Alert Threshold                    |\n   233→|---------------------------|--------------------|------------------------------------|\n   234→| `http_request_duration`   | < 150 ms p95       | > 250 ms for 5 min                 |\n   235→| `grpc_error_rate`         | < 0.2 %            | > 5 % rolling 1 min                |\n   236→| `redis_cache_hit_ratio`   | > 0.9              | < 0.7 rolling 15 min               |\n   237→| `jetstream_lag`           | < 1 msg           | > 50 msg for 30 s                  |\n   238→\n   239→Logs are shipped to **ELK**, traces to **Jaeger**, metrics scraped by **Prometheus** and rendered in **Grafana** dashboards (`dashboards/` folder).\n   240→\n   241→---\n   242→\n   243→## 8. Security Posture\n   244→\n   245→* **mTLS** between services (`SPIFFE` identities).  \n   246→* **OPA** sidecar on gateway for fine-grained policy.  \n   247→* Every JWT carries:\n   248→  - `cid` (canvas-id) – tenant sharding\n   249→  - `scopes` – `palette:rw`, `texture:r`  \n   250→  - `aud` (service audience)  \n   251→\n   252→*Rate limiting* – token-bucket (Redis) keyed by `sub:cid`.\n   253→\n   254→---\n   255→\n   256→## 9. Failure Modes & Resilience\n   257→\n   258→| Scenario                        | Mitigation                                  |\n   259→|---------------------------------|---------------------------------------------|\n   260→| Network partition               | gRPC retries + circuit-breaker (Hystrix-c)  |\n   261→| Gateway overload                | Adaptive LRU cache, request shedder         |\n   262→| Service crash                   | Kubernetes restart, health probes           |\n   263→| DB connection pool exhaustion   | PgBouncer & slow-query logger               |\n   264→| Upstream incompatible version   | Dual-run old/new schema + feature flags     |\n   265→\n   266→---\n   267→\n   268→## 10. Deployment Pipeline\n   269→\n   270→1. **Pre-merge** – `clang-tidy`, `cppcheck`, `unit-tests` (CTEST).  \n   271→2. **Build** – `docker build --platform=linux/amd64 --target runtime`.  \n   272→3. **Security** – `trivy fs`, SBOM via `syft`.  \n   273→4. **Canary** – 5 % traffic for 30 min, monitored saturation SLOs.  \n   274→5. **Promotion** – Git tag + Helm release (`charts/`).  \n   275→\n   276→---\n   277→\n   278→## 11. Glossary\n   279→\n   280→| Term       | Meaning                                                    |\n   281→|------------|------------------------------------------------------------|\n   282→| Brush-Stroke | Individual API call altering the live canvas state.       |\n   283→| Swatch       | A semantically-named color token (`#FF22AA`, “ultra-violet”). |\n   284→| Texture Node | Procedural fragment generating a 2D/3D texture.          |\n   285→| Narrative Arc| Branch in story graph triggered by user input.           |\n   286→\n   287→---\n   288→\n   289→## 12. References\n   290→\n   291→* _Reactive Design Patterns_ – R. Kuhn  \n   292→* _Clean Architecture in C_ – A. Vision\n\nSynestheticCanvas transforms every HTTP or WebSocket request into a _brush-stroke_ on a living digital canvas.  \nMicroservices supply the colors, textures, and motion, while the **GraphQL/REST gateway** orchestrates them into a single, time-synchronous response.  \nThe architecture has three non-negotiable goals:\n\n1. **Expressiveness** – Artists can combine services like mixing paint.  \n2. **Evolution** – New art forms (e.g., volumetric audio) can be added without breaking existing exhibitions.  \n3. **Performance** – Frame-accurate (< 16 ms) responses even under festival-scale load.\n\n---\n\n## 2. Macro-Level Topology\n\n```mermaid\nflowchart LR\n    subgraph Client Edge\n        VR[VR Headset]\n        Web[Web Frontend]\n        Bot[AI Bot]\n    end\n\n    subgraph Gateway Layer\n        GQL(GraphQL Gateway)\n        REST(REST Fallback)\n    end\n\n    subgraph Core Services\n        Palette(Palette Svc)\n        Synth(Texture Synth)\n        Audio(Audio-React)\n        Narr(Narrative Engine)\n    end\n    \n    subgraph Shared Infra\n        LogStack(ELK/OTel)\n        Redis((Redis Cache))\n        MQ((NATS JetStream))\n        PG[(PostgreSQL Cluster)]\n    end\n\n    VR -->|Query / Mutation| GQL\n    Web --> GQL\n    Bot --> REST\n    \n    GQL -- gRPC/json --> Palette & Synth & Audio & Narr\n    REST -- HTTP--> Palette & Synth & Audio & Narr\n    \n    GQL -- sub/ms logs --> LogStack\n    REST -- prometheus --> LogStack\n    \n    Palette --- PG\n    Synth --- Redis\n    Audio --- MQ\n    Narr  --- PG\n\n    classDef red fill:#ffdfe0,stroke:#ce2b37,stroke-width:2;\n    class REST red\n```\n\n---\n\n## 3. Layered Breakdown\n\n| Layer                | Responsibility                                           | Key Patterns                        |\n|----------------------|----------------------------------------------------------|-------------------------------------|\n| **Gateway**          | Auth, rate-limit, schema stitching, cache-aside          | API Gateway, GraphQL Schema         |\n| **Service Layer**    | Domain orchestrations, streaming, CQRS                   | Command/Query Separation            |\n| **Repository Layer** | Data access, DAO mapping, migrations                     | Repository Pattern                  |\n| **Infra**            | Observability, tracing, message bus, persistence         | Sidecar + 12-Factor                 |\n\n---\n\n## 4. Gateway Internals\n\n### 4.1 GraphQL Route Lifecycle\n\n```mermaid\nsequenceDiagram\n    participant C as Client\n    participant G as Gateway\n    participant V as Validator\n    participant S as Service\n    participant L as Logger\n    \n    C->>G: gql { mutateTexture(...) }\n    G->>V: schema.validate(request)\n    V->>G: Ok / Err\n    alt Err\n        G->>C: 422 Unprocessable Entity\n        G->>L: warn(validation)\n    else Ok\n        G->>S: gRPC call (texture-synth)\n        S-->>G: TexturePayload\n        G->>L: info(request)\n        G->>C: 200 + JSON\n    end\n```\n\n#### 4.1.1 Validation Rules\n* **Static** – schema-compiled (enum ranges, non-null fields)  \n* **Dynamic** – cross-service (`audioId` exists, `paletteId` owns user)  \n* **Security** – depth limit, cost analysis, JWT claims scoping  \n\n### 4.2 REST Fallback\n\n* Code-generated from the GraphQL schema using `gnostic` + custom templates.  \n* Follows HATEOAS, supports `?page[limit]=`, `?page[offset]=` for pagination.  \n\n---\n\n## 5. Versioning Strategy\n\n1. Breaking changes → new _major_ (e.g., `v2`) schema namespace.  \n2. Additive changes → patch bump (`v1.3 → v1.4`) and feature flag.  \n3. Deprecated fields emit `Deprecation-Notice` headers for 90 days.  \n\n```\nquery v1_3 {\n  palette(id: \"ultra-violet\") {          # valid\n    swatches { hex }                     \n  }\n}\n\nquery v2_0 {\n  paletteGet(id: \"ultra-violet\") {       # renamed\n    swatches { hex, rgb }                # new field\n  }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 6. Service Blueprint (C Reference)\n\nA microservice is a standalone **statically-linked** ELF built with **C17** and a thin **gRPC** layer.  \nBelow is a simplified excerpt from `palette_svc/src/service.c`.\n\n```c\n/**\n * @file service.c\n * @brief Palette microservice – Service entry point & request handlers.\n */\n\n#include <stdlib.h>\n#include <signal.h>\n#include \"core/palette.h\"\n#include \"infra/logger.h\"\n#include \"infra/repository.h\"\n#include \"gen/palette.grpc.pb-c.h\"\n\nstatic palette_repo_t *repo = NULL;\n\n/* gRPC → C callback ------------------------------------------------------- */\nstatic void handle_palette_get(\n        const PaletteGetRequest *req,\n        PaletteGetReply **resp,\n        void *user_data)\n{\n    (void)user_data;\n    sc_trace_span_t span = sc_trace_start(\"palette_get\");\n\n    /* 1. Validate -------------------------------- */\n    if (!req->id || req->id[0] == '\\0') {\n        sc_log_warn(\"palette_get: empty id\");\n        *resp = palette_get_reply__error(\n            HTTP_BAD_REQUEST, \"Palette ID must not be empty\");\n        goto finish;\n    }\n\n    /* 2. Repository ------------------------------ */\n    palette_t pal = {0};\n    sc_error_t err = repo->get(repo, req->id, &pal);\n    if (err.code == SC_ERR_NOT_FOUND) {\n        *resp = palette_get_reply__error(\n            HTTP_NOT_FOUND, \"Palette not found\");\n        goto finish;\n    } else if (err.code != SC_OK) {\n        sc_log_error(\"repo->get failed: %s\", err.msg);\n        *resp = palette_get_reply__error(\n            HTTP_INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR, \"Internal error\");\n        goto finish;\n    }\n\n    /* 3. Mapping → ProtoBuf ---------------------- */\n    *resp = palette_get_reply__success(&pal);\n\nfinish:\n    sc_trace_end(&span);\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char **argv)\n{\n    sc_logger_init_default();          /* JSON-structured logs          */\n    sc_trace_init_otel();              /* OpenTelemetry exporter        */\n\n    repo = palette_repo_init(getenv(\"PG_DSN\"));\n    if (!repo) {\n        sc_log_fatal(\"failed to init repository\");\n        return EXIT_FAILURE;\n    }\n\n    sc_grpc_server_t *srv = sc_grpc_server_create(\"0.0.0.0:7001\");\n    sc_grpc_server_register_palette_get(srv, handle_palette_get, NULL);\n\n    /* Graceful shutdown ------------------------- */\n    signal(SIGTERM, sc_grpc_server_stop);\n    signal(SIGINT,  sc_grpc_server_stop);\n\n    sc_log_info(\"palette_svc listening on :7001\");\n    sc_grpc_server_serve(srv);\n\n    palette_repo_free(repo);\n    sc_grpc_server_free(srv);\n    sc_logger_flush();\n    return EXIT_SUCCESS;\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 7. Observability & Monitoring\n\n| Metric                    | Target             | Alert Threshold                    |\n|---------------------------|--------------------|------------------------------------|\n| `http_request_duration`   | < 150 ms p95       | > 250 ms for 5 min                 |\n| `grpc_error_rate`         | < 0.2 %            | > 5 % rolling 1 min                |\n| `redis_cache_hit_ratio`   | > 0.9              | < 0.7 rolling 15 min               |\n| `jetstream_lag`           | < 1 msg           | > 50 msg for 30 s                  |\n\nLogs are shipped to **ELK**, traces to **Jaeger**, metrics scraped by **Prometheus** and rendered in **Grafana** dashboards (`dashboards/` folder).\n\n---\n\n## 8. Security Posture\n\n* **mTLS** between services (`SPIFFE` identities).  \n* **OPA** sidecar on gateway for fine-grained policy.  \n* Every JWT carries:\n  - `cid` (canvas-id) – tenant sharding\n  - `scopes` – `palette:rw`, `texture:r`  \n  - `aud` (service audience)  \n\n*Rate limiting* – token-bucket (Redis) keyed by `sub:cid`.\n\n---\n\n## 9. Failure Modes & Resilience\n\n| Scenario                        | Mitigation                                  |\n|---------------------------------|---------------------------------------------|\n| Network partition               | gRPC retries + circuit-breaker (Hystrix-c)  |\n| Gateway overload                | Adaptive LRU cache, request shedder         |\n| Service crash                   | Kubernetes restart, health probes           |\n| DB connection pool exhaustion   | PgBouncer & slow-query logger               |\n| Upstream incompatible version   | Dual-run old/new schema + feature flags     |\n\n---\n\n## 10. Deployment Pipeline\n\n1. **Pre-merge** – `clang-tidy`, `cppcheck`, `unit-tests` (CTEST).  \n2. **Build** – `docker build --platform=linux/amd64 --target runtime`.  \n3. **Security** – `trivy fs`, SBOM via `syft`.  \n4. **Canary** – 5 % traffic for 30 min, monitored saturation SLOs.  \n5. **Promotion** – Git tag + Helm release (`charts/`).  \n\n---\n\n## 11. Glossary\n\n| Term       | Meaning                                                    |\n|------------|------------------------------------------------------------|\n| Brush-Stroke | Individual API call altering the live canvas state.       |\n| Swatch       | A semantically-named color token (`#FF22AA`, “ultra-violet”). |\n| Texture Node | Procedural fragment generating a 2D/3D texture.          |\n| Narrative Arc| Branch in story graph triggered by user input.           |\n\n---\n\n## 12. References\n\n* _Reactive Design Patterns_ – R. Kuhn  \n* _Clean Architecture in C_ – A. Martin  \n* GraphQL Spec `2021-10`  \n* OpenTelemetry v1.26.0  \n\n---\n\n> © 2024 SynestheticCanvas Consortium – Released under the Apache 2.0 License\n```","numLines":299,"startLine":1,"totalLines":299}}}

Architecture  \n     3→*File: `SynestheticCanvas/docs/architecture/microservices.md`*  \n     4→*Project: **api_graphql***  \n     5→\n     6→> The following document is part reference, part living with graceful shutdown paths  \n    17→\n    18→\n    19→---\n    20→\n    21→## 1  Service Topology\n    22→\n    23→```\n    24→           ┌─────────────┐            ┌──────────────┐\n    25→           │ Palette MS  │────────────▶│ Texture  MS  │\n    26→           └──────┬──────┘            └──────┬───────┘\n    27→                  │                           │\n    28→                  │ └───────────────┘\n    32→                  │\n    33→                  ▼\n    34→           ┌──────────────┐\n    35→           │  API GW      │  (GraphQL+REST, caching, RBAC)\n    36→           └──────────────┘\n    37→```\n    38→\n    39→Each micro-service encapsulates exactly one creative concern and  \n    40→communicates through HTTP/2 + Protocol Buffers over gRPC *internally*  \n    41→and GraphQL/REST *externally*.\n    42→\n    43→---\n    44→\n    45→## 2  Common Library: `sc_core`\n    46→\n    47→All services link against a minimal abstraction library that standardises\n    48→logging, config, tracing, JSON and Protobuf helpers.\n    49→\n    50→```c\n    51→/* File: sc_core/logger.h */\n    52→#ifndef SC_CORE_LOGGER_H\n    53→#define SC_CORE_LOGGER_H\n    54→\n    55→#include <stdio.h>\n    56→#include <time.h>\n    57→\n    58→typedef enum {\n    59→    SC_LOG_DEBUG,\n    60→    SC_LOG_INFO,\n    61→    SC_LOG_WARN,\n    62→    SC_LOG_ERROR\n    63→} sc_log_level_t;\n    64→\n    65→/**\n    66→ * Initialise the global logger.\n    67→ * @param level Minimum level that will be emitted.\n    68→ * @return 0 on success, -1 on failure (errno is set).\n    69→ */\n    70→int sc_log_init(sc_log_level_t level);\n    71→\n    72→/**\n    73→ * Log a formatted message. Always thread-safe.\n    74→ */\n    75→void sc_log(sc_log_level_t level, const char *fmt, ...);\n    76→\n    77→/* Convenience macros */\n    78→#define SC_LOGD(...) sc_log(SC_LOG_DEBUG, __VA_ARGS__)\n    79→#define SC_LOGI(...) sc_log(SC_LOG_INFO,  __VA_ARGS__)\n    80→#define SC_LOGW(...) sc_log(SC_LOG_WARN,  __VA_ARGS__)\n    81→#define SC_LOGE(...) sc_log(SC_LOG_ERROR, __VA_ARGS__)\n    82→\n    83→#endif /* SC_CORE_LOGGER_H */\n    84→```\n    85→\n    86→```c\n    87→/* File: sc_core/logger.c */\n    88→#define _POSIX_C_SOURCE 200809L\n    89→#include \"logger.h\"\n    90→#include <stdarg.h>\n    91→#include <pthread.h>\n    92→#include <string.h>\n    93→\n    94→static struct {\n    95→    sc_log_level_t min_level;\n    96→    pthread_mutex_t mtx;\n    97→    FILE *stream;\n    98→} g_ctx = { .min_level = SC_LOG_INFO, .mtx = PTHREAD_MUTEX_INITIALIZER,\n    99→            .stream = NULL };\n   100→\n   101→static const char *level_to_str(sc_log_level_t lvl) {\n   102→    switch (lvl) {\n   103→        case SC_LOG_DEBUG: return \"DEBUG\";\n   104→        case SC_LOG_INFO:  return \"INFO \";\n   105→        case SC_LOG_WARN:  return \"WARN \";\n   106→        default:           return \"ERROR\";\n   107→    }\n   108→}\n   109→\n   110→int sc_log_init(sc_log_level_t level) {\n   111→    g_ctx.min_level = level;\n   112→    g_ctx.stream = stderr;\n   113→    return 0;\n   114→}\n   115→\n   116→void sc_log(sc_log_level_t level, const char *fmt, ...) {\n   117→    if (level < g_ctx.min_level) return;\n   118→\n   119→    time_t now = time(NULL);\n   120→    struct tm tm_now;\n   121→    localtime_r(&now, &tm_now);\n   122→\n   123→    char ts[32];\n   124→    strftime(ts, sizeof ts, \"%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S\", &tm_now);\n   125→\n   126→    pthread_mutex_lock(&g_ctx.mtx);\n   127→    fprintf(g_ctx.stream, \"%s [%s] \", ts, level_to_str(level));\n   128→\n   129→    va_list ap;\n   130→    va_start(ap, fmt);\n   131→    vfprintf(g_ctx.stream, fmt, ap);\n   132→    va_end(ap);\n   133→\n   134→    fputc('\\n', g_ctx.stream);\n   135→    fflush(g_ctx.stream);\n   136→    pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_ctx.mtx);\n   137→}\n   138→```\n   139→\n   140→---\n   141→\n   142→## 3  Canonical Service Template\n   143→\n   144→Every new service clones this template, wires its domain logic, and registers\n   145→with Consul for discovery.\n   146→\n   147→```c\n   148→/* File: svc_template/main.c */\n   149→#define _DEFAULT_SOURCE\n   150→#include <microhttpd.h>\n   151→#include <signal.h>\n   152→#include <stdlib.h>\n   153→#include \"sc_core/logger.h\"\n   154→#include \"router.h\"\n   155→\n   156→#define PORT 8080\n   157→\n   158→static volatile sig_atomic_t running = 1;\n   159→\n   160→static void handle_sig(int sig) {\n   161→    (void)sig;\n   162→    running = 0;\n   163→}\n   164→\n   165→int main(void) {\n   166→    /* 1. Initialise cross-cutting concerns */\n   167→    sc_log_init(SC_LOG_DEBUG);\n   168→\n   169→    SC_LOGI(\"Starting Palette Service v1.2.0\");\n   170→\n   171→    /* 2. Configure graceful termination */\n   172→    struct sigaction sa = { .sa_handler = handle_sig };\n   173→    sigaction(SIGINT, &sa, NULL);\n   174→    sigaction(SIGTERM, &sa, NULL);\n   175→\n   176→    /* 3. Start HTTP daemon */\n   177→    struct MHD_Daemon *daemon = MHD_start_daemon(\n   178→        MHD_USE_SELECT_INTERNALLY | MHD_USE_EPOLL_INTERNALLY,\n   179→        PORT, NULL, NULL, &router_dispatch, NULL,\n   180→        MHD_OPTION_CONNECTION_TIMEOUT,  (unsigned int)10,\n   181→        MHD_OPTION_END);\n   182→\n   183→    if (!daemon) {\n   184→        SC_LOGE(\"Failed to start HTTP daemon\");\n   185→        return EXIT_FAILURE;\n   186→    }\n   187→\n   188→    /* 4. Main loop */\n   189→    while (running) {\n   190→        sleep(1);\n   191→    }\n   192→\n   193→    /* 5. Shutdown */\n   194→    MHD_stop_daemon(daemon);\n   195→    SC_LOGI(\"Shutdown complete\");\n   196→    return EXIT_SUCCESS;\n   197→}\n   198→```\n   199→\n   200→`router_dispatch` is generated by a compile-time code-gen step that turns an\n   201→OpenAPI spec (`openapi.yaml`) and a GraphQL schema (`schema.graphql`) into a\n   202→multipart dispatcher.  \n   203→For brevity, we hand-write a minimal implementation below.\n   204→\n   205→---\n   206→\n   207→## 4  Case Study – Palette Management Service\n   208→\n   209→### 4.1  Domain Model\n   210→\n   211→```c\n   212→/* File: palette/model.h */\n   213→#ifndef PALETTE_MODEL_H\n   214→#define PALETTE_MODEL_H\n   215→\n   216→#include <stdint.h>\n   217→\n   218→typedef struct {\n   219→    uint32_t id;\n   220→    char     name[64];\n   221→    uint8_t  r, g, b, a;\n   222→} palette_t;\n   223→\n   224→#endif /* PALETTE_MODEL_H */\n   225→```\n   226→\n   227→### 4.2  Repository Layer\n   228→\n   229→```c\n   230→/* File: palette/repository.h */\n   231→#ifndef PALETTE_REPOSITORY_H\n   232→#define PALETTE_REPOSITORY_H\n   233→\n   234→#include \"model.h\"\n   235→#include <stddef.h>\n   236→\n   237→/**\n   238→ * Create or update a palette. Returns 0 on success.\n   239→ */\n   240→int palette_repo_save(const palette_t *p);\n   241→\n   242→/**\n   243→ * Retrieve a palette by ID.\n   244→ * @return 0 on success, -ENOENT if not found.\n   245→ */\n   246→int palette_repo_get(uint32_t id, palette_t *out);\n   247→\n   248→/**\n   249→ * Paginated list of palettes.\n   250→ * @param offset Zero-based.\n   251→ * @param limit  Max items.\n   252→ * @param out    Pre-allocated buffer.\n   253→ * @return number of items written, or negative on error.\n   254→ */\n   255→ssize_t palette_repo_list(size_t offset, size_t limit, palette_t *out);\n   256→\n   257→#endif /* PALETTE_REPOSITORY_H */\n   258→```\n   259→\n   260→```c\n   261→/* File: palette/repository_sqlite.c */\n   262→#define _GNU_SOURCE\n   263→#include \"repository.h\"\n   264→#include \"sc_core/logger.h\"\n   265→#include <sqlite3.h>\n   266→#include <errno.h>\n   267→\n   268→static sqlite3 *db = NULL;\n   269→\n   270→static int ensure_db(void) {\n   271→    if (db) return 0;\n   272→    int rc = sqlite3_open(\"data/palette.db\", &db);\n   273→    if (rc != SQLITE_OK) {\n   274→        SC_LOGE(\"SQLite open error: %s\", sqlite3_errmsg(db));\n   275→        return -EIO;\n   276→    }\n   277→    char *err = NULL;\n   278→    const char *ddl =\n   279→        \"CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS palette (\"\n   280→        \"id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\"\n   281→        \"name TEXT NOT NULL,\"\n   282→        \"r INTEGER NOT NULL,\"\n   283→        \"g INTEGER NOT NULL,\"\n   284→        \"b INTEGER NOT NULL,\"\n   285→        \"a INTEGER NOT NULL);\";\n   286→    rc = sqlite3_exec(db, ddl, NULL, NULL, &err);\n   287→    if (rc != SQLITE_OK) {\n   288→        SC_LOGE(\"SQLite DDL error: %s\", err);\n   289→        sqlite3_free(err);\n   290→        return -EIO;\n   291→    }\n   292→    return 0;\n   293→}\n   294→\n   295→int palette_repo_save(const palette_t *p) {\n   296→    if (!p) return -EINVAL;\n   297→    if (ensure_db() < 0) return -EIO;\n   298→\n   299→    const char *sql =\n   300→        \"INSERT INTO palette(id, name, r, g, b, a) \"\n   301→        \"VALUES(?1, ?2, ?3, ?4, ?5, ?6) \"\n   302→        \"ON CONFLICT(id) DO UPDATE SET \"\n   303→        \"name=excluded.name, r=excluded.r, g=excluded.g, \"\n   304→        \"b=excluded.b, a=excluded.a;\";\n   305→\n   306→    sqlite3_stmt *stmt = NULL;\n   307→    if (sqlite3_prepare_v2(db, sql, -1, &stmt, NULL) != SQLITE_OK) {\n   308→        SC_LOGE(\"SQLite prepare error: %s\", sqlite3_errmsg(db));\n   309→        return -EIO;\n   310→    }\n   311→\n   312→    sqlite3_bind_int   (stmt, 1, p->id);\n   313→    sqlite3_bind_text  (stmt, 2, p->name, -1, SQLITE_TRANSIENT);\n   314→    sqlite3_bind_int   (stmt, 3, p->r);\n   315→    sqlite3_bind_int   (stmt, 4, p->g);\n   316→    sqlite3_bind_int   (stmt, 5, p->b);\n   317→    sqlite3_bind_int   (stmt, 6, p->a);\n   318→\n   319→    int rc = sqlite3_step(stmt);\n   320→    sqlite3_finalize(stmt);\n   321→    return (rc == SQLITE_DONE) ? 0 : -EIO;\n   322→}\n   323→\n   324→int palette_repo_get(uint32_t id, palette_t *out) {\n   325→    if (!out) return -EINVAL;\n   326→    if (ensure_db() < 0) return -EIO;\n   327→\n   328→    const char *sql = \"SELECT id, name, r, g, b, a FROM palette WHERE id=?1;\";\n   329→    sqlite3_stmt *stmt = NULL;\n   330→    if (sqlite3_prepare_v2(db, sql, -1, &stmt, NULL) != SQLITE_OK) {\n   331→        SC_LOGE(\"SQLite prepare error: %s\", sqlite3_errmsg(db));\n   332→        return -EIO;\n   333→    }\n   334→    sqlite3_bind_int(stmt, 1, id);\n   335→\n   336→    int rc = sqlite3_step(stmt);\n   337→    if (rc == SQLITE_ROW) {\n   338→        out->id   = sqlite3_column_int(stmt, 0);\n   339→        strncpy(out->name, (const char *)sqlite3_column_text(stmt, 1), sizeof out->name);\n   340→        out->r    = sqlite3_column_int(stmt, 2);\n   341→        out->g    = sqlite3_column_int(stmt, 3);\n   342→        out->b    = sqlite3_column_int(stmt, 4);\n   343→        out->a    = sqlite3_column_int(stmt, 5);\n   344→        sqlite3_finalize(stmt);\n   345→        return 0;\n   346→    }\n   347→    sqlite3_finalize(stmt);\n   348→    return -ENOENT;\n   349→}\n   350→\n   351→ssize_t palette_repo_list(size_t offset, size_t limit, palette_t *out) {\n   352→    if (!out || !limit) return -EINVAL;\n   353→    if (ensure_db() < 0) return -EIO;\n   354→\n   355→    const char *sql =\n   356→        \"SELECT id, name, r, g, b, a FROM palette \"\n   357→        \"ORDER BY id LIMIT ?1 OFFSET ?2;\";\n   358→    sqlite3_stmt *stmt = NULL;\n   359→    if (sqlite3_prepare_v2(db, sql, -1, &stmt, NULL) != SQLITE_OK) {\n   360→        SC_LOGE(\"SQLite prepare error: %s\", sqlite3_errmsg(db));\n   361→        return -EIO;\n   362→    }\n   363→    sqlite3_bind_int(stmt, 1, limit);\n   364→    sqlite3_bind_int(stmt, 2, offset);\n   365→\n   366→    ssize_t count = 0;\n   367→    while (sqlite3_step(stmt) == SQLITE_ROW && count < (ssize_t)limit) {\n   368→        palette_t *p = &out[count++];\n   369→        p->id = sqlite3_column_int(stmt, 0);\n   370→        strncpy(p->name, (const char *)sqlite3_column_text(stmt, 1), sizeof p->name);\n   371→        p->r = sqlite3_column_int(stmt, 2);\n   372→        p->g = sqlite3_column_int(stmt, 3);\n   373→        p->b = sqlite3_column_int(stmt, 4);\n   374→        p->a = sqlite3_column_int(stmt, 5);\n   375→    }\n   376→    sqlite3_finalize(stmt);\n   377→    return count;\n   378→}\n   379→```\n   380→\n   381→### 4.3  REST + GraphQL Router\n   382→\n   383→```c\n   384→/* File: svc_template/router.c */\n   385→#include <microhttpd.h>\n   386→#include \"sc_core/logger.h\"\n   387→#include \"palette/repository.h\"\n   388→#include <cjson/cJSON.h>\n   389→#include <string.h>\n   390→\n   391→static int json_response(struct MHD_Connection *conn,\n   392→                         const cJSON *json, int status) {\n   393→    char *payload = cJSON_PrintUnformatted(json);\n   394→    struct MHD_Response *resp =\n   395→        MHD_create_response_from_buffer(strlen(payload), payload,\n   396→                                        MHD_RESPMEM_MUST_FREE);\n   397→    if (!resp) return MHD_NO;\n   398→\n   399→    MHD_add_response_header(resp, \"Content-Type\", \"application/json\");\n   400→    int ret = MHD_queue_response(conn, status, resp);\n   401→    MHD_destroy_response(resp);\n   402→    return ret;\n   403→}\n   404→\n   405→static int handle_get_palette(struct MHD_Connection *conn,\n   406→                              uint32_t id) {\n   407→    palette_t p;\n   408→    if (palette_repo_get(id, &p) != 0) {\n   409→        cJSON *err = cJSON_CreateObject();\n   410→        cJSON_AddStringToObject(err, \"error\", \"Palette not found\");\n   411→        return json_response(conn, err, MHD_HTTP_NOT_FOUND);\n   412→    }\n   413→\n   414→    cJSON *json = cJSON_CreateObject();\n   415→    cJSON_AddNumberToObject(json, \"id\",   p.id);\n   416→    cJSON_AddStringToObject(json, \"name\", p.name);\n   417→    cJSON_AddNumberToObject(json, \"r\",    p.r);\n   418→    cJSON_AddNumberToObject(json, \"g\",    p.g);\n   419→    cJSON_AddNumberToObject(json, \"b\",    p.b);\n   420→    cJSON_AddNumberToObject(json, \"a\",    p.a);\n   421→    return json_response(conn, json, MHD_HTTP_OK);\n   422→}\n   423→\n   424→int router_dispatch(void *cls,\n   425→                    struct MHD_Connection *connection,\n   426→                    const char *url, const char *method,\n   427→                    const char *ver, const char *upload_data,\n   428→                    size_t *upload_data_size, void **con_cls) {\n   429→    (void)cls; (void)ver; (void)upload_data; (void)upload_data_size; (void)con_cls;\n   430→\n   431→    SC_LOGD(\"Incoming %s %s\", method, url);\n   432→\n   433→    /* Example route: GET /v1/palette/<id> */\n   434→    if (strcmp(method, \"GET\") == 0 &&\n   435→        strncmp(url, \"/v1/palette/\", 12) == 0) {\n   436→\n   437→        uint32_t id = (uint32_t)strtoul(url + 12, NULL, 10);\n   438→        return handle_get_palette(connection, id);\n   439→    }\n   440→\n   441→    /* Default 404 */\n   442→    cJSON *err = cJSON_CreateObject();\n   443→    cJSON_AddStringToObject(err, \"error\", \"Route not found\");\n   444→    return json_response(connection, err, MHD_HTTP_NOT_FOUND);\n   445→}\n   446→```\n   447→\n   448→---\n   449→\n   450→## 5  GraphQL Gateway Stub\n   451→\n   452→The gateway composes service schemas using Apollo Federation syntax\n   453→and delegates sub-queries through the internal gRPC mesh.  \n   454→Below is a trimmed skeleton that wraps our Palette service.\n   455→\n   456→```c\n   457→/* File: gateway/resolvers/palette_resolver.c */\n   458→#include \"sc_core/logger.h\"\n   459→#include <grpc/grpc.h>\n   460→#include <grpc/byte_buffer.h>\n   461→#include \"generated/palette.grpc.pb-c.h\"\n   462→\n   463→bool gql_resolve_palette(struct gql_query_ctx *ctx) {\n   464→    uint32_t id = gql_arg_u32(ctx, \"id\");\n   465→    Palette__GetRequest req = PALETTE__GET_REQUEST__INIT;\n   466→    req.id = id;\n   467→\n   468→    /* Send over gRPC */\n   469→    grpc_channel *ch = grpc_insecure_channel_create(\"palette:50051\", NULL, NULL);\n   470→    grpc_call *call = grpc_channel_create_call(\n   471→        ch, NULL, GRPC_PROPAGATE_DEFAULT, ctx->cq,\n   472→        grpc_slice_from_static_string(\"/palette.Palette/Get\"),\n   473→        NULL, gpr_inf_future(GPR_CLOCK_REALTIME), NULL);\n   474→\n   475→    grpc_metadata_array md_arr;\n   476→    grpc_metadata_array_init(&md_arr);\n   477→\n   478→    grpc_byte_buffer *payload =\n   479→        grpc_raw_byte_buffer_create(NULL, 0);\n   480→\n   481→    grpc_op ops[6];\n   482→    memset(ops, 0, sizeof ops);\n   483→    ops[0].op = GRPC_OP_SEND_INITIAL_METADATA;\n   484→    ops[0].data.send_initial_metadata.count = 0;\n   485→\n   486→    ops[1].op = GRPC_OP_SEND_MESSAGE;\n   487→    ops[1].data.send_message.send_message = payload;\n   488→\n   489→    ops[2].op = GRPC_OP_SEND_CLOSE_FROM_CLIENT;\n   490→\n   491→    ops[3].op = GRPC_OP_RECV_INITIAL_METADATA;\n   492→    ops[3].data.recv_initial_metadata.recv_initial_metadata = &md_arr;\n   493→\n   494→    grpc_status_code status;\n   495→    grpc_slice details;\n   496→    ops[4].op = GRPC_OP_RECV_STATUS_ON_CLIENT;\n   497→    ops[4].data.recv_status_on_client.status = &status;\n   498→    ops[4].data.recv_status_on_client.status_details = &details;\n   499→\n   500→    grpc_call_error cerr = grpc_call_start_batch(call, ops, 5, NULL, NULL);\n   501→    if (cerr != GRPC_CALL_OK) {\n   502→        SC_LOGE(\"gRPC start batch failed\");\n   503→        return false;\n   504→    }\n   505→\n   506→    grpc_completion_queue_pluck(ctx->cq, NULL, gpr_inf_future(GPR_CLOCK_REALTIME), NULL);\n   507→\n   508→    /* Transform Protobuf to GraphQL JSON */\n   509→    if (status != GRPC_STATUS_OK) {\n   510→        gql_error(ctx, \"Palette service error\");\n   511→        return false;\n   512→    }\n   513→\n   514→    /* Omitted: decode response into ctx->response */\n   515→\n   516→    grpc_metadata_array_destroy(&md_arr);\n   517→    grpc_byte_buffer_destroy(payload);\n   518→    grpc_call_destroy(call);\n   519→    grpc_channel_destroy(ch);\n   520→    return true;\n   521→}\n   522→```\n   523→\n   524→---\n   525→\n   526→## 6  Versioning & Lifecycle\n   527→\n   528→- Every service exposes `/version` returning `{ \"semver\": \"x.y.z\", \"git\": \"<sha>\" }`.\n   529→- GraphQL schemas are versioned via `@link(import:)` directives.\n   530→- REST endpoints embed a major version in the URL (`/v1/`, `/v2/`).\n   531→- Obsolete routes remain available for *one* minor version with deprecation\n   532→  warnings in the response headers.\n   533→\n   534→---\n   535→\n   536→## 7  Observability & Resilience\n   537→\n   538→1. **Structured logging** (`JSON` when `LOG_FORMAT=json` env var is set).  \n   539→2. **Metrics** exported via `/metrics` in Prometheus format.  \n   540→3. **Tracing** instrumented through OpenTelemetry C SDK.  \n   541→4. **Circuit-breaker** wrapper auto-generated around every gRPC stub.  \n   542→5. **Rate-limiting** handled at the API Gateway using the Token-Bucket\n   543→   algorithm (Redis backend).\n   544→\n   545→---\n   546→\n   547→## 8  Build & Deployment\n   548→\n   549→```\n   550→$ mkdir build && cd build\n   551→$ cmake -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release ..\n   552→$ make -j$(nproc)\n   553→$ docker build -t synesthetic/palette:1.2.0 services/palette\n   554→$ helm upgrade --install palette charts/palette\n   555→```\n   556→\n   557→CI jobs run `clang-tidy`, `cppcheck`, unit tests (cmocka), and integration\n   558→tests spun up with `testcontainers-c`.\n   559→\n   560→---\n   561→\n   562→## 9  Appendix – Compile Flags\n   563→\n   564→```\n   565→-std=c17\n   566→-Wall SynestheticCanvas Micro-services Architecture  \n*File: `SynestheticCanvas/docs/architecture/microservices.md`*  \n*Project: **api_graphql***  \n\n> The following document is part reference, part living specification.  \n> All code blocks are **production-ready, self-contained C modules** that\n> can be compiled and deployed as-is (barring obvious path changes).  \n> To keep the document future-proof, every sample follows the same\n> conventions you will find across the entire SynestheticCanvas code-base:\n>\n> • C17 standard  \n> • No global state except for well-defined singletons  \n> • `-Wall -Wextra -Werror` clean  \n> • Strict separation between I/O, domain logic, and data access  \n> • Independently testable units with graceful shutdown paths  \n\n\n---\n\n## 1  Service Topology\n\n```\n           ┌─────────────┐            ┌──────────────┐\n           │ Palette MS  │────────────▶│ Texture  MS  │\n           └──────┬──────┘            └──────┬───────┘\n                  │                           │\n                  │                           │\n           ┌──────▼───────┐          ┌───────▼───────┐\n           │ Narrative MS │──────────▶│ Audio MS     │\n           └──────┬───────┘          └───────────────┘\n                  │\n                  ▼\n           ┌──────────────┐\n           │  API GW      │  (GraphQL+REST, caching, RBAC)\n           └──────────────┘\n```\n\nEach micro-service encapsulates exactly one creative concern and  \ncommunicates through HTTP/2 + Protocol Buffers over gRPC *internally*  \nand GraphQL/REST *externally*.\n\n---\n\n## 2  Common Library: `sc_core`\n\nAll services link against a minimal abstraction library that standardises\nlogging, config, tracing, JSON and Protobuf helpers.\n\n```c\n/* File: sc_core/logger.h */\n#ifndef SC_CORE_LOGGER_H\n#define SC_CORE_LOGGER_H\n\n#include <stdio.h>\n#include <time.h>\n\ntypedef enum {\n    SC_LOG_DEBUG,\n    SC_LOG_INFO,\n    SC_LOG_WARN,\n    SC_LOG_ERROR\n} sc_log_level_t;\n\n/**\n * Initialise the global logger.\n * @param level Minimum level that will be emitted.\n * @return 0 on success, -1 on failure (errno is set).\n */\nint sc_log_init(sc_log_level_t level);\n\n/**\n * Log a formatted message. Always thread-safe.\n */\nvoid sc_log(sc_log_level_t level, const char *fmt, ...);\n\n/* Convenience macros */\n#define SC_LOGD(...) sc_log(SC_LOG_DEBUG, __VA_ARGS__)\n#define SC_LOGI(...) sc_log(SC_LOG_INFO,  __VA_ARGS__)\n#define SC_LOGW(...) sc_log(SC_LOG_WARN,  __VA_ARGS__)\n#define SC_LOGE(...) sc_log(SC_LOG_ERROR, __VA_ARGS__)\n\n#endif /* SC_CORE_LOGGER_H */\n```\n\n```c\n/* File: sc_core/logger.c */\n#define _POSIX_C_SOURCE 200809L\n#include \"logger.h\"\n#include <stdarg.h>\n#include <pthread.h>\n#include <string.h>\n\nstatic struct {\n    sc_log_level_t min_level;\n    pthread_mutex_t mtx;\n    FILE *stream;\n} g_ctx = { .min_level = SC_LOG_INFO, .mtx = PTHREAD_MUTEX_INITIALIZER,\n            .stream = NULL };\n\nstatic const char *level_to_str(sc_log_level_t lvl) {\n    switch (lvl) {\n        case SC_LOG_DEBUG: return \"DEBUG\";\n        case SC_LOG_INFO:  return \"INFO \";\n        case SC_LOG_WARN:  return \"WARN \";\n        default:           return \"ERROR\";\n    }\n}\n\nint sc_log_init(sc_log_level_t level) {\n    g_ctx.min_level = level;\n    g_ctx.stream = stderr;\n    return 0;\n}\n\nvoid sc_log(sc_log_level_t level, const char *fmt, ...) {\n    if (level < g_ctx.min_level) return;\n\n    time_t now = time(NULL);\n    struct tm tm_now;\n    localtime_r(&now, &tm_now);\n\n    char ts[32];\n    strftime(ts, sizeof ts, \"%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S\", &tm_now);\n\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&g_ctx.mtx);\n    fprintf(g_ctx.stream, \"%s [%s] \", ts, level_to_str(level));\n\n    va_list ap;\n    va_start(ap, fmt);\n    vfprintf(g_ctx.stream, fmt, ap);\n    va_end(ap);\n\n    fputc('\\n', g_ctx.stream);\n    fflush(g_ctx.stream);\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&g_ctx.mtx);\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 3  Canonical Service Template\n\nEvery new service clones this template, wires its domain logic, and registers\nwith Consul for discovery.\n\n```c\n/* File: svc_template/main.c */\n#define _DEFAULT_SOURCE\n#include <microhttpd.h>\n#include <signal.h>\n#include <stdlib.h>\n#include \"sc_core/logger.h\"\n#include \"router.h\"\n\n#define PORT 8080\n\nstatic volatile sig_atomic_t running = 1;\n\nstatic void handle_sig(int sig) {\n    (void)sig;\n    running = 0;\n}\n\nint main(void) {\n    /* 1. Initialise cross-cutting concerns */\n    sc_log_init(SC_LOG_DEBUG);\n\n    SC_LOGI(\"Starting Palette Service v1.2.0\");\n\n    /* 2. Configure graceful termination */\n    struct sigaction sa = { .sa_handler = handle_sig };\n    sigaction(SIGINT, &sa, NULL);\n    sigaction(SIGTERM, &sa, NULL);\n\n    /* 3. Start HTTP daemon */\n    struct MHD_Daemon *daemon = MHD_start_daemon(\n        MHD_USE_SELECT_INTERNALLY | MHD_USE_EPOLL_INTERNALLY,\n        PORT, NULL, NULL, &router_dispatch, NULL,\n        MHD_OPTION_CONNECTION_TIMEOUT,  (unsigned int)10,\n        MHD_OPTION_END);\n\n    if (!daemon) {\n        SC_LOGE(\"Failed to start HTTP daemon\");\n        return EXIT_FAILURE;\n    }\n\n    /* 4. Main loop */\n    while (running) {\n        sleep(1);\n    }\n\n    /* 5. Shutdown */\n    MHD_stop_daemon(daemon);\n    SC_LOGI(\"Shutdown complete\");\n    return EXIT_SUCCESS;\n}\n```\n\n`router_dispatch` is generated by a compile-time code-gen step that turns an\nOpenAPI spec (`openapi.yaml`) and a GraphQL schema (`schema.graphql`) into a\nmultipart dispatcher.  \nFor brevity, we hand-write a minimal implementation below.\n\n---\n\n## 4  Case Study – Palette Management Service\n\n### 4.1  Domain Model\n\n```c\n/* File: palette/model.h */\n#ifndef PALETTE_MODEL_H\n#define PALETTE_MODEL_H\n\n#include <stdint.h>\n\ntypedef struct {\n    uint32_t id;\n    char     name[64];\n    uint8_t  r, g, b, a;\n} palette_t;\n\n#endif /* PALETTE_MODEL_H */\n```\n\n### 4.2  Repository Layer\n\n```c\n/* File: palette/repository.h */\n#ifndef PALETTE_REPOSITORY_H\n#define PALETTE_REPOSITORY_H\n\n#include \"model.h\"\n#include <stddef.h>\n\n/**\n * Create or update a palette. Returns 0 on success.\n */\nint palette_repo_save(const palette_t *p);\n\n/**\n * Retrieve a palette by ID.\n * @return 0 on success, -ENOENT if not found.\n */\nint palette_repo_get(uint32_t id, palette_t *out);\n\n/**\n * Paginated list of palettes.\n * @param offset Zero-based.\n * @param limit  Max items.\n * @param out    Pre-allocated buffer.\n * @return number of items written, or negative on error.\n */\nssize_t palette_repo_list(size_t offset, size_t limit, palette_t *out);\n\n#endif /* PALETTE_REPOSITORY_H */\n```\n\n```c\n/* File: palette/repository_sqlite.c */\n#define _GNU_SOURCE\n#include \"repository.h\"\n#include \"sc_core/logger.h\"\n#include <sqlite3.h>\n#include <errno.h>\n\nstatic sqlite3 *db = NULL;\n\nstatic int ensure_db(void) {\n    if (db) return 0;\n    int rc = sqlite3_open(\"data/palette.db\", &db);\n    if (rc != SQLITE_OK) {\n        SC_LOGE(\"SQLite open error: %s\", sqlite3_errmsg(db));\n        return -EIO;\n    }\n    char *err = NULL;\n    const char *ddl =\n        \"CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS palette (\"\n        \"id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\"\n        \"name TEXT NOT NULL,\"\n        \"r INTEGER NOT NULL,\"\n        \"g INTEGER NOT NULL,\"\n        \"b INTEGER NOT NULL,\"\n        \"a INTEGER NOT NULL);\";\n    rc = sqlite3_exec(db, ddl, NULL, NULL, &err);\n    if (rc != SQLITE_OK) {\n        SC_LOGE(\"SQLite DDL error: %s\", err);\n        sqlite3_free(err);\n        return -EIO;\n    }\n    return 0;\n}\n\nint palette_repo_save(const palette_t *p) {\n    if (!p) return -EINVAL;\n    if (ensure_db() < 0) return -EIO;\n\n    const char *sql =\n        \"INSERT INTO palette(id, name, r, g, b, a) \"\n        \"VALUES(?1, ?2, ?3, ?4, ?5, ?6) \"\n        \"ON CONFLICT(id) DO UPDATE SET \"\n        \"name=excluded.name, r=excluded.r, g=excluded.g, \"\n        \"b=excluded.b, a=excluded.a;\";\n\n    sqlite3_stmt *stmt = NULL;\n    if (sqlite3_prepare_v2(db, sql, -1, &stmt, NULL) != SQLITE_OK) {\n        SC_LOGE(\"SQLite prepare error: %s\", sqlite3_errmsg(db));\n        return -EIO;\n    }\n\n    sqlite3_bind_int   (stmt, 1, p->id);\n    sqlite3_bind_text  (stmt, 2, p->name, -1, SQLITE_TRANSIENT);\n    sqlite3_bind_int   (stmt, 3, p->r);\n    sqlite3_bind_int   (stmt, 4, p->g);\n    sqlite3_bind_int   (stmt, 5, p->b);\n    sqlite3_bind_int   (stmt, 6, p->a);\n\n    int rc = sqlite3_step(stmt);\n    sqlite3_finalize(stmt);\n    return (rc == SQLITE_DONE) ? 0 : -EIO;\n}\n\nint palette_repo_get(uint32_t id, palette_t *out) {\n    if (!out) return -EINVAL;\n    if (ensure_db() < 0) return -EIO;\n\n    const char *sql = \"SELECT id, name, r, g, b, a FROM palette WHERE id=?1;\";\n    sqlite3_stmt *stmt = NULL;\n    if (sqlite3_prepare_v2(db, sql, -1, &stmt, NULL) != SQLITE_OK) {\n        SC_LOGE(\"SQLite prepare error: %s\", sqlite3_errmsg(db));\n        return -EIO;\n    }\n    sqlite3_bind_int(stmt, 1, id);\n\n    int rc = sqlite3_step(stmt);\n    if (rc == SQLITE_ROW) {\n        out->id   = sqlite3_column_int(stmt, 0);\n        strncpy(out->name, (const char *)sqlite3_column_text(stmt, 1), sizeof out->name);\n        out->r    = sqlite3_column_int(stmt, 2);\n        out->g    = sqlite3_column_int(stmt, 3);\n        out->b    = sqlite3_column_int(stmt, 4);\n        out->a    = sqlite3_column_int(stmt, 5);\n        sqlite3_finalize(stmt);\n        return 0;\n    }\n    sqlite3_finalize(stmt);\n    return -ENOENT;\n}\n\nssize_t palette_repo_list(size_t offset, size_t limit, palette_t *out) {\n    if (!out || !limit) return -EINVAL;\n    if (ensure_db() < 0) return -EIO;\n\n    const char *sql =\n        \"SELECT id, name, r, g, b, a FROM palette \"\n        \"ORDER BY id LIMIT ?1 OFFSET ?2;\";\n    sqlite3_stmt *stmt = NULL;\n    if (sqlite3_prepare_v2(db, sql, -1, &stmt, NULL) != SQLITE_OK) {\n        SC_LOGE(\"SQLite prepare error: %s\", sqlite3_errmsg(db));\n        return -EIO;\n    }\n    sqlite3_bind_int(stmt, 1, limit);\n    sqlite3_bind_int(stmt, 2, offset);\n\n    ssize_t count = 0;\n    while (sqlite3_step(stmt) == SQLITE_ROW && count < (ssize_t)limit) {\n        palette_t *p = &out[count++];\n        p->id = sqlite3_column_int(stmt, 0);\n        strncpy(p->name, (const char *)sqlite3_column_text(stmt, 1), sizeof p->name);\n        p->r = sqlite3_column_int(stmt, 2);\n        p->g = sqlite3_column_int(stmt, 3);\n        p->b = sqlite3_column_int(stmt, 4);\n        p->a = sqlite3_column_int(stmt, 5);\n    }\n    sqlite3_finalize(stmt);\n    return count;\n}\n```\n\n### 4.3  REST + GraphQL Router\n\n```c\n/* File: svc_template/router.c */\n#include <microhttpd.h>\n#include \"sc_core/logger.h\"\n#include \"palette/repository.h\"\n#include <cjson/cJSON.h>\n#include <string.h>\n\nstatic int json_response(struct MHD_Connection *conn,\n                         const cJSON *json, int status) {\n    char *payload = cJSON_PrintUnformatted(json);\n    struct MHD_Response *resp =\n        MHD_create_response_from_buffer(strlen(payload), payload,\n                                        MHD_RESPMEM_MUST_FREE);\n    if (!resp) return MHD_NO;\n\n    MHD_add_response_header(resp, \"Content-Type\", \"application/json\");\n    int ret = MHD_queue_response(conn, status, resp);\n    MHD_destroy_response(resp);\n    return ret;\n}\n\nstatic int handle_get_palette(struct MHD_Connection *conn,\n                              uint32_t id) {\n    palette_t p;\n    if (palette_repo_get(id, &p) != 0) {\n        cJSON *err = cJSON_CreateObject();\n        cJSON_AddStringToObject(err, \"error\", \"Palette not found\");\n        return json_response(conn, err, MHD_HTTP_NOT_FOUND);\n    }\n\n    cJSON *json = cJSON_CreateObject();\n    cJSON_AddNumberToObject(json, \"id\",   p.id);\n    cJSON_AddStringToObject(json, \"name\", p.name);\n    cJSON_AddNumberToObject(json, \"r\",    p.r);\n    cJSON_AddNumberToObject(json, \"g\",    p.g);\n    cJSON_AddNumberToObject(json, \"b\",    p.b);\n    cJSON_AddNumberToObject(json, \"a\",    p.a);\n    return json_response(conn, json, MHD_HTTP_OK);\n}\n\nint router_dispatch(void *cls,\n                    struct MHD_Connection *connection,\n                    const char *url, const char *method,\n                    const char *ver, const char *upload_data,\n                    size_t *upload_data_size, void **con_cls) {\n    (void)cls; (void)ver; (void)upload_data; (void)upload_data_size; (void)con_cls;\n\n    SC_LOGD(\"Incoming %s %s\", method, url);\n\n    /* Example route: GET /v1/palette/<id> */\n    if (strcmp(method, \"GET\") == 0 &&\n        strncmp(url, \"/v1/palette/\", 12) == 0) {\n\n        uint32_t id = (uint32_t)strtoul(url + 12, NULL, 10);\n        return handle_get_palette(connection, id);\n    }\n\n    /* Default 404 */\n    cJSON *err = cJSON_CreateObject();\n    cJSON_AddStringToObject(err, \"error\", \"Route not found\");\n    return json_response(connection, err, MHD_HTTP_NOT_FOUND);\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5  GraphQL Gateway Stub\n\nThe gateway composes service schemas using Apollo Federation syntax\nand delegates sub-queries through the internal gRPC mesh.  \nBelow is a trimmed skeleton that wraps our Palette service.\n\n```c\n/* File: gateway/resolvers/palette_resolver.c */\n#include \"sc_core/logger.h\"\n#include <grpc/grpc.h>\n#include <grpc/byte_buffer.h>\n#include \"generated/palette.grpc.pb-c.h\"\n\nbool gql_resolve_palette(struct gql_query_ctx *ctx) {\n    uint32_t id = gql_arg_u32(ctx, \"id\");\n    Palette__GetRequest req = PALETTE__GET_REQUEST__INIT;\n    req.id = id;\n\n    /* Send over gRPC */\n    grpc_channel *ch = grpc_insecure_channel_create(\"palette:50051\", NULL, NULL);\n    grpc_call *call = grpc_channel_create_call(\n        ch, NULL, GRPC_PROPAGATE_DEFAULT, ctx->cq,\n        grpc_slice_from_static_string(\"/palette.Palette/Get\"),\n        NULL, gpr_inf_future(GPR_CLOCK_REALTIME), NULL);\n\n    grpc_metadata_array md_arr;\n    grpc_metadata_array_init(&md_arr);\n\n    grpc_byte_buffer *payload =\n        grpc_raw_byte_buffer_create(NULL, 0);\n\n    grpc_op ops[6];\n    memset(ops, 0, sizeof ops);\n    ops[0].op = GRPC_OP_SEND_INITIAL_METADATA;\n    ops[0].data.send_initial_metadata.count = 0;\n\n    ops[1].op = GRPC_OP_SEND_MESSAGE;\n    ops[1].data.send_message.send_message = payload;\n\n    ops[2].op = GRPC_OP_SEND_CLOSE_FROM_CLIENT;\n\n    ops[3].op = GRPC_OP_RECV_INITIAL_METADATA;\n    ops[3].data.recv_initial_metadata.recv_initial_metadata = &md_arr;\n\n    grpc_status_code status;\n    grpc_slice details;\n    ops[4].op = GRPC_OP_RECV_STATUS_ON_CLIENT;\n    ops[4].data.recv_status_on_client.status = &status;\n    ops[4].data.recv_status_on_client.status_details = &details;\n\n    grpc_call_error cerr = grpc_call_start_batch(call, ops, 5, NULL, NULL);\n    if (cerr != GRPC_CALL_OK) {\n        SC_LOGE(\"gRPC start batch failed\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    grpc_completion_queue_pluck(ctx->cq, NULL, gpr_inf_future(GPR_CLOCK_REALTIME), NULL);\n\n    /* Transform Protobuf to GraphQL JSON */\n    if (status != GRPC_STATUS_OK) {\n        gql_error(ctx, \"Palette service error\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    /* Omitted: decode response into ctx->response */\n\n    grpc_metadata_array_destroy(&md_arr);\n    grpc_byte_buffer_destroy(payload);\n    grpc_call_destroy(call);\n    grpc_channel_destroy(ch);\n    return true;\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 6  Versioning & Lifecycle\n\n- Every service exposes `/version` returning `{ \"semver\": \"x.y.z\", \"git\": \"<sha>\" }`.\n- GraphQL schemas are versioned via `@link(import:)` directives.\n- REST endpoints embed a major version in the URL (`/v1/`, `/v2/`).\n- Obsolete routes remain available for *one* minor version with deprecation\n  warnings in the response headers.\n\n---\n\n## 7  Observability & Resilience\n\n1. **Structured logging** (`JSON` when `LOG_FORMAT=json` env var is set).  \n2. **Metrics** exported via `/metrics` in Prometheus format.  \n3. **Tracing** instrumented through OpenTelemetry C SDK.  \n4. **Circuit-breaker** wrapper auto-generated around every gRPC stub.  \n5. **Rate-limiting** handled at the API Gateway using the Token-Bucket\n   algorithm (Redis backend).\n\n---\n\n## 8  Build & Deployment\n\n```\n$ mkdir build && cd build\n$ cmake -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release ..\n$ make -j$(nproc)\n$ docker build -t synesthetic/palette:1.2.0 services/palette\n$ helm upgrade --install palette charts/palette\n```\n\nCI jobs run `clang-tidy`, `cppcheck`, unit tests (cmocka), and integration\ntests spun up with `testcontainers-c`.\n\n---\n\n## 9  Appendix – Compile Flags\n\n```\n-std=c17\n-Wall -Wextra   SC_IPC_EIO            = -4,   /* I/O error – see errno for details             */\n    SC_IPC_EPROTO         = -5,   /* Protocol violation / corrupt frame            */\n    SC_IPC_ECLOSED        = value.        */\nconst char *sc_ipc_strerr(sc_ipc_err_t err);\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*  Message envelope                                                         */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\ntypedef enum sc_ipc_msg_type\n{\n    SC_IPC_MSG_REQUEST    = 1,\n    SC_IPC_MSG_RESPONSE   = 2,\n    SC_IPC_MSG_EVENT      = 3,\n    SC_IPC_MSG_HEARTBEAT  = 4\n} sc_ipc_msg_type_t;\n\n/*\n * The envelope precedes every payload.  All integer fields are encoded in the\n * native endianness of the host (the IPC is local-host only).  The envelope is\n * always transmitted in a single write(2)/send(2) call to guarantee atomicity\n * on UNIX domain sockets for payloads < PIPE_BUF – see POSIX.1-2001.\n */\n#pragma pack(push, 1)\ntypedef struct sc_ipc_msg_hdr\n{\n    uint32_t         magic;          /* == SC_IPC_MSG_MAGIC                       */\n    uint16_t         version;        /* Envelope format version (currently 1)     */\n    uint16_t         type;           /* sc_ipc_msg_type_t                         */\n    uint32_t         length;         /* Length of following payload in bytes      */\n    uint64_t         correlation_id; /* Client-supplied token for matchmaking      */\n    uint64_t         timestamp_ns;   /* CLOCK_REALTIME timestamp, nanoseconds     */\n} sc_ipc_msg_hdr_t;\n#pragma pack(pop)\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*  IPC handle (opaque to callers)                                           */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\ntypedef struct sc_ipc_channel\n{\n    int              fd;                       /* Socket descriptor            */\n    bool             is_server;                /* Server or client side?       */\n    char             endpoint[SC_IPC_MAX_ENDPOINT + 1];\n} sc_ipc_channel_t;\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*  Initialization / teardown                                                */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*\n * Initialize a listening server socket at `endpoint` (UNIX-domain path).\n * Returns SC_IPC_SUCCESS on success, otherwise a negative error code.\n */\nint sc_ipc_server_init(sc_ipc_channel_t      *srv,\n                       const char            *endpoint,\n                       size_t                 backlog);\n\n/*\n * Initialize a client socket and connect to the specified `endpoint`.\n * `timeout_ms` ∈ [0, UINT32_MAX]  (0 → block indefinitely).\n */\nint sc_ipc_client_init(sc_ipc_channel_t      *cli,\n                       const char            *endpoint,\n                       uint32_t               timeout_ms);\n\n/*\n * Accept an incoming client connection on `srv` into `out_cli`.\n * On success returns SC_IPC_SUCCESS and `out_cli` is initialized.\n */\nint sc_ipc_accept(const sc_ipc_channel_t     *srv,\n                  sc_ipc_channel_t           *out_cli,\n                  uint32_t                    timeout_ms);\n\n/* Close socket and wipe internal state (safe to call on uninitialized obj). */\nvoid sc_ipc_close(sc_ipc_channel_t *chan);\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*  Message I/O                                                              */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*\n * Send a payload with envelope fields filled automatically.  The function\n * blocks up to `timeout_ms`;  0 → blocking, UINT32_MAX → poll once + return.\n */\nint sc_ipc_send_msg(sc_ipc_channel_t        *chan,\n                    sc_ipc_msg_type_t         type,\n                    const void              *payload,\n                    size_t                    payload_len,\n                    uint64_t                  correlation_id,\n                    uint32_t                  timeout_ms);\n\n/*\n * Receive a message into `buf` (size `buf_size`) and write header to `hdr`.\n * On success returns the number of payload bytes copied into `buf`. Returns 0 on success. */\nstatic int\nwrite_full(int fd, const uint8_t *buf, size_t len)\n{\n    while (len > 0) {\n        ssize_t n = write(fd, buf, len);\n        if (n < 0) {\n            if (errno == EINTR)\n                continue;\n            if (errno == EAGAIN || errno == EWOULDBLOCK) {\n                /* Busy-wait could be replaced with poll/epoll for high-load\n                 * services. while (len > 0) {\n        int rc = poll(&pfd, 1, deadline_ms);\n        if (rc == 0)\n            return -2; /* timeout                               */\n        if (rc < 0) {\n            if (errno == EINTR)\n                continue;\n            return -1;\n        }\n        ssize_t n = read(fd, buf, len);\n        if (n == 0)\n            return -1; /* orderly shutdown (unexpected here) -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\nstatic size_t curl_buffer_write(void *contents, size_t size, size_t nmemb, void *userp)\n{\n    size_t realsize    = size * nmemb;\n    curl_buffer_t *mem = (curl_buffer_t *)userp;\n\n    char *ptr = realloc(mem->data, mem->size + realsize + 1);\n    if (!ptr) {\n        /* Out of memory – abort transfer */\n        return 0;\n    }\n\n    mem->data = ptr;\n    memcpy(&(mem->data[mem->size]), contents, realsize);\n    mem->size += realsize;\n    mem->data[mem->size] = '\\0';\n\n    return realsize;\n}\n\n/* Perform an HTTP GET and parse the JSON payload. */\nstatic json_t *http_json_get(const char *url, logger_t *log, GQLError **gql_err)\n{\n    CURL *curl = curl_easy_init();\n    if (!curl) {\n .size = 0};\n\n    curl_easy_setopt(curl, CURLOPT_URL, url);\n    curl_easy_setopt(curl, CURLOPT_WRITEFUNCTION, curl_buffer_write);\n    curl_easy_setopt(curl, CURLOPT_WRITEDATA, (void *)&chunk);\n    curl_easy_setopt(curl, CURLOPT_TIMEOUT, HTTP_TIMEOUT_SECS);\n    curl_easy_setopt(curl, CURLOPT_USERAGENT, \"SynestheticCanvas-Gateway/1.0\");\n\n    CURLcode res = curl_easy_perform(curl);\n    long http_status = 0;\n    curl_easy_getinfo(curl, CURLINFO_RESPONSE_CODE, &http_status);\n    curl_easy_cleanup(curl);\n\n    if (res != CURLE_OK) {\n        LOG_ERROR(log, \"curl_easy_perform() failed: %s\", curl_easy_strerror(res));\n        free(chunk.data);\n        *gql_err = gql_error_new(GQL_ERR_NETWORK, \"Upstream network error\");\n        return NULL;\n    }\n\n    if (http_status >= 400) \"Resource not found\");\n        } else {\n            *gql_err = gql_error_new(GQL_ERR_UPSTREAM, \"Upstream service error\");\n        }\n        return NULL;\n    }\n\n    /* Parse JSON payload */\n    json_error_t json_err;\n    json_t *json = json_loads(chunk.data, 0, &json_err);\n    free(chunk.data);\n\n    if (!json) {\n        LOG_ERROR(log, \"JSON parse error on line %d: %s\", json_err.line, json_err.text);\n        *gql_err = gql_error_new(GQL_ERR_INTERNAL, \"Invalid upstream JSON\");\n        return NULL;\n    }\n\n    return json;\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                                Validators                                  */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n/* Validate a UUID argument (simple length/format check). */\nstatic int validate_uuid_arg(const char *uuid, GQLError UUID\");\n        return -1;\n    }\n    return 0;\n}\n\n/* Extract and validate pagination arguments. */\nstatic int extract_pagination(const json_t *args,\n                              size_t if (first_json) *out_first = (size_t)json_integer_value(first_json);\n    if (after_json) *out_after = json_string_value(after_json);\n\n    return 0;\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                          Resolver implementations                          */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n/**\n * palette(id: ID!): Palette!\n */\nstatic json_t *resolve_palette(const json_t *args, void *user_data, GQLError --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\noption (SC_ENABLE_TESTS        \"Build unit/integration tests\"                       ON)\noption (SC_ENABLE_SANITIZERS   \"Enable compile-time sanitizers (Debug only)\"        ON)\noption (SC_ENABLE_COVERAGE     \"Enable coverage instrumentation (GCC/Clang, Debug)\" OFF)\noption (SC_BUILD_SHARED        \"Build shared library instead of executable\"         OFF)\noption (SC_WARNINGS_AS_ERRORS  \"Treat compiler warnings as errors\"                  ON)\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n#  Toolchain & Compilation\n# strictness\nif (CMAKE_C_COMPILER_ID MATCHES \"GNU|Clang\")\n    add_compile_options (\n        -Wall -Wextra -Wpedantic\n        -Wcast-align -Wpointer-arith -Wstrict-prototypes\n        -Wmissing-prototypes -Wformat=2 -Wshadow\n    )\n    if (SC_WARNINGS_AS_ERRORS)\n        add_compile_options (-Werror)\n    endif ()\nelseif (MSVC)\n    add_compile_options (/W4 /permissive-)\nendif ()\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n#  Version auto-discovery from git (overrides project() version if available)\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Generates include/sc_version.h with:\n#     #define SC_API_GQL_VERSION \"1.4.0+g<commit>\"\n#     #define SC_API_GQL_GIT_HASH \"<commit>\"\n#     #define SC_API_GQL_GIT_BRANCH \"<branch>\"\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\nfind_package (Git QUIET)\n\nif (GIT_FOUND AND EXISTS \"${PROJECT_SOURCE_DIR}/.git\")\n ()\n\nconfigure_file (\n    ${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/cmake/templates/sc_version.h.in\n    ${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/generated/sc_version.h\n    @ONLY\n)\n\ninclude_directories (${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/generated)\ninstall (FILES ${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/generated/sc_version.h\n        DESTINATION include/synesthetic_canvas)\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n#  Dependencies\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\ninclude (FetchContent)\n\n# 1. cJSON – Lightweight JSON parser/serializer\nFetchContent_Declare (\n    cJSON\n    GIT_REPOSITORY https://github.com/DaveGamble/cJSON.git\n    GIT_TAG        v1.7.16\n)\nFetchContent_MakeAvailable (cJSON)\n\n# 2. libgraphqlparser – Facebook’s GraphQL parser (C bindings)\n#    Pre-built packages may not exist everywhere, so fallback to FetchContent\nfind_package (graphqlparser QUIET)\nif (NOT graphqlparser_FOUND)\n    message (STATUS \"libgraphqlparser not found, fetching from source…\")\n    FetchContent_Declare (\n        graphqlparser\n        GIT_REPOSITORY https://github.com/graphql/libgraphqlparser.git\n        GIT_TAG        0.7.2\n    )\n    set (BUILD_SHARED_LIBS OFF CACHE BOOL \"\" FORCE)        # Build static internally\n    FetchContent_MakeAvailable (graphqlparser)\nendif ()\n\n# 3. libuv – Event loop / async I/O OpenSSL (for TLS if we add HTTPS listener)\nfind_package (OpenSSL 1.1 REQUIRED)\n\n# 5. zlib (for compressed payloads)\nfind_package (ZLIB REQUIRED)\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n#  Source layout\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# We keep a flat list for clarity; you may generate this automatically with filesystem globbing, but\n# explicit listing is preferred for reproducible builds.\nset (SC_API_GQL_SRC\n    src/main.c\n    src/http_server.c\n    src/http_server.h\n    src/graphql_router.c\n    src/graphql_router.h\n    src/validators.c\n    src/validators.h\n    src/pagination.c\n    src/pagination.h\n    src/logging.c\n    src/logging.h\n    src/metrics.c\n    src/metrics.h\n    src/config.c\n    src/config.h\n)\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n#  Build target\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\nif (SC_BUILD_SHARED)\n    add_library      (${SC_API_GQL_TARGET} SHARED ${SC_API_GQL_SRC})\n    set_target_properties (${SC_API_GQL_TARGET}\n        PROPERTIES SOVERSION ${SC_API_GQL_SOVERSION})\nelse ()\n    add_executable   (${SC_API_GQL_TARGET} ${SC_API_GQL_SRC})\nendif ()\n\ntarget_include_directories (${SC_API_GQL_TARGET}\n    PUBLIC  $<BUILD_INTERFACE:${PROJECT_SOURCE_DIR}/include>\n            $<INSTALL_INTERFACE:include>\n)\n\ntarget_link_libraries (${SC_API_GQL_TARGET}\n    PRIVATE\n        cjson\n        graphqlparser\n        uv\n        OpenSSL::SSL\n        ZLIB::ZLIB\n)\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n#  Sanitizers (clang/gcc, Debug only)\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\nif (SC_ENABLE_SANITIZERS AND CMAKE_BUILD_TYPE STREQUAL \"Debug\")\n    if (CMAKE_C_COMPILER_ID MATCHES \"GNU|Clang\")\n        target_compile_options (${SC_API_GQL_TARGET} PRIVATE -fsanitize=address,undefined -fno-omit-frame-pointer)\n        target_link_options    (${SC_API_GQL_TARGET} PRIVATE -fsanitize=address,undefined)\n    endif ()\nendif ()\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n#  Coverage\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\nif (SC_ENABLE_COVERAGE AND CMAKE_C_COMPILER_ID MATCHES \"GNU|Clang\")\n    target_compile_options (${SC_API_GQL_TARGET} PRIVATE -O0 -g --coverage)\n    target_link_options    (${SC_API_GQL_TARGET} PRIVATE --coverage)\nendif ()\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n#  Install rules\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\ninclude (GNUInstallDirs)\n\ninstall (TARGETS ${SC_API_GQL_TARGET}\n        EXPORT  SynestheticCanvasTargets\n        RUNTIME DESTINATION ${CMAKE_INSTALL_BINDIR}\n        LIBRARY DESTINATION ${CMAKE_INSTALL_LIBDIR}\n        ARCHIVE DESTINATION ${CMAKE_INSTALL_LIBDIR}\n)\n\ninstall ${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/synesthetic_canvas_api_graphql.pc\n        DESTINATION ${CMAKE_INSTALL_LIBDIR}/pkgconfig)\n\n#  CMake package config for find_package()\ninstall (EXPORT service_port;      /* default 0 means \"use environment variable\"   */\n\n    /* feature toggles */\n    bool             enable_monitoring;\n    bool             enable_validation;\n    bool             enable_request_tracing;\n\n    /* runtime knobs */\n    sc_log_level_t   log_level;\n    uint32_t         max_payload_size;\n    uint16_t         default_page_size;\n} sc_runtime_config_t;\n\n/*==========================================================================*/\n/*  Inline Helpers                                                          */\n/*==========================================================================*/\n#include <stdlib.h>\n#include <string.h>\n\nSC_PUBLIC\nstatic inline uint16_t sc_u16_from_env(const char *name, uint16_t fallback)\n{\n    const char *val = getenv(name);\n    if (!val || !*val) return fallback;\n\n    char *endptr = NULL;\n    unsigned long parsed = strtoul(val, &endptr, 10);\n    if (endptr == val || *endptr != '\\0' || parsed > 0xFFFFUL)\n        return fallback;\n\n    return (uint16_t)parsed;\n}\n\nSC_PUBLIC\nstatic inline bool sc_bool_from_env(const char *name, bool fallback)\n{\n    const char *val = getenv(name);\n    if (!val || !*val) return fallback;\n\n    if (!strcasecmp(val, \"1\") ||\n        !strcasecmp(val, \"true\") ||\n        !strcasecmp(val, \"yes\") ||\n        !strcasecmp(val, \"on\"))\n        return true;\n\n    if (!strcasecmp(val, \"0\") ||\n        !strcasecmp(val, \"false\") ||\n        !strcasecmp(val, \"no\") ||\n        !strcasecmp(val, \"off\"))\n        return false;\n\n    return fallback;\n}\n\nSC_PUBLIC\nstatic inline sc_log_level_t sc_log_level_from_env(const char *name, sc_log_level_t fallback)\n{\n    const char *val = getenv(name);\n    if (!val) return fallback;\n\n    if (!strcasecmp(val, \"trace\")) return SC_LOG_TRACE;\n    if (!strcasecmp(val, \"debug\")) return SC_LOG_DEBUG;\n    if (!strcasecmp(val, \"info\"))  return SC_LOG_INFO;\n    if (!strcasecmp(val, \"warn\"))  return SC_LOG_WARN;\n    if (!strcasecmp(val, \"error\")) return SC_LOG_ERROR;\n    if (!strcasecmp(val, \"fatal\")) return SC_LOG_FATAL;\n\n    return fallback;\n}\n\n/*-------------------------------------------------------------------------\n * sc_config_load_default\n *\n * Populate config with sane defaults, reading from well-known getenv(\"SC_ENVIRONMENT\") : \"development\";\n    cfg->service_name          = service_name;\n    cfg->service_port          = sc_u16_from_env(\"SC_SERVICE_PORT\", default_port);\n\n    cfg->enable_monitoring     = sc_bool_from_env(\"SC_ENABLE_MONITORING\", SC_FEATURE_MONITORING);\n    cfg->enable_validation     = sc_bool_from_env(\"SC_ENABLE_VALIDATION\", SC_FEATURE_VALIDATION);\n    cfg->enable_request_tracing= sc_bool_from_env(\"SC_ENABLE_TRACING\",    false);\n\n    cfg->log_level             = sc_log_level_from_env(\"SC_LOG_LEVEL\", (sc_log_level_t)SC_LOG_LEVEL);\n    cfg->max_payload_size      = (uint32_t)strtoul(getenv(\"SC_MAX_PAYLOAD\") ?: \"\", NULL, 10);\n    if (cfg->max_payload_size == 0 || cfg->max_payload_size > SC_MAX_PAYLOAD_SIZE)\n        cfg->max_payload_size = SC_MAX_PAYLOAD_SIZE;\n\n    cfg->default_page_size     = (uint16_t)strtoul(getenv(\"SC_DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE\") ?: \"\", NULL, 10);\n    if (cfg->default_page_size == 0 || cfg->default_page_size > 1000)\n        cfg->default_page_size = SC_DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE;\n}\n\n/*==========================================================================*/\n/*  Deprecation / Unavailable                                               */\n/*==========================================================================*/\n#if SC_COMPILER_GCC || (__has_attribute(deprecated))\n#   define SC_DEPRECATED(msg) __attribute__((deprecated(msg)))\n#elif SC_COMPILER_MSVC\n#   define SC_DEPRECATED(msg) __declspec(deprecated(msg))\n#else\n#   define SC_DEPRECATED(msg)\n#endif\n\n#ifdef __cplusplus\n} /* extern \"C\" */\n#endif\n\n#endif /* SC_CONFIG_H */\n","numLines":303,"startLine":1,"totalLines":303}}}
