Quick Start\n\n```bash\n# Clone & build the full constellation\ngit clone --recurse-submodules https://github.com/synestheticlabs/synesthetic-canvas.git\ncd synesthetic-canvas/SynestheticCanvas/api-gateway\nmkdir build && cd build\ncmake -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release ..\nmake -j$(nproc)\n\n# Run gateway with default in-container config\n./sc-api-gateway --config ../config/gateway.toml\n```\n\nThe gateway listens by default on:\n\n| Protocol | Port | Purpose                   |\n|----------|------|---------------------------|\n| HTTP/1.1 | 8080 | REST fallback endpoints   |\n| HTTP/2   | 8443 | GraphQL over TLS (gRPC)   |\n| UDP      | 8125 | Metrics (StatsD/Datadog)  |\n\n---\n\n## 2. Minimal Client Example (C17)\n\nBelow is a self-contained example that queries the `currentPalette` GraphQL endpoint with pagination & version headers.  \nBuild with: `cc -Wall -Wextra -pedantic -std=c17 client.c -lcurl -o client`\n\n```c\n/**\n * @file client.c\n * @brief Example consumer of SynestheticCanvas API Gateway.\n *\n * Demonstrates:\n *  - GraphQL query composition in C\n *  - HTTP/2 request with libcurl\n *  - Basic error handling\n *  - Versioned schema negotiation\n */\n#include <stdio.h>\n#include <stdlib.h>\n#include <string.h>\n#include <curl/curl.h>\n\n/* Convenience structure for dynamic buffer */\ntypedef struct {\n    char   *data;\n    size_t  size;\n} buffer_t;\n\n/* libcurl write-back to dynamic buffer */\nstatic size_t on_write(char *ptr, size_t size, size_t nmemb, void *userdata)\n{\n    size_t    bytes = size * nmemb;\n    buffer_t *buf   = userdata;\n\n    char *new_data = realloc(buf->data, buf->size + bytes + 1);\n    if (!new_data) return 0; /* abort transfer */\n\n    buf->data = new_data;\n    memcpy(buf->data + buf->size, ptr, bytes);\n    buf->size += bytes;\n    buf->data[buf->size] = '\\0';\n    return bytes;\n}\n\n/* Utility: fatal error & exit */\nstatic void die(const char *msg, CURLcode code)\n{\n    fprintf(stderr, \"ERROR: %s (%s)\\n\", msg, curl_easy_strerror(code));\n    exit(EXIT_FAILURE);\n}\n\nint main(void)\n{\n    static const char *graphql_query =\n        \"{\"\n        \"  currentPalette(page: 1, perPage: 10) {\"\n        \"    id\"\n        \"    name\"\n        \"    colors { hex }\"\n        \"    updatedAt\"\n        \"  }\"\n        \"}\";\n\n    /* JSON body with query */\n    char json_body[1024];\n    snprintf(json_body, sizeof(json_body),\n             \"{\\\"query\\\":\\\"%s\\\"}\", graphql_query);\n\n    /* Initialize curl */\n    CURL *curl = curl_easy_init();\n    if (!curl) {\n        fputs(\"Unable to init curl\\n\", stderr);\n        return EXIT_FAILURE;\n    }\n\n    buffer_t buf = { .data = NULL, .size = 0 };\n\n    /* REST URL fallback; change to https://localhost:8443/graphql for TLS */\n    curl_easy_setopt(curl, CURLOPT_URL, \"http://localhost:8080/graphql\");\n    curl_easy_setopt(curl, CURLOPT_HTTP_VERSION, CURL_HTTP_VERSION_2_0);\n    curl_easy_setopt(curl, CURLOPT_POST, 1L);\n    curl_easy_setopt(curl, CURLOPT_POSTFIELDS, json_body);\n    curl_easy_setopt(curl, CURLOPT_WRITEFUNCTION, on_write);\n    curl_easy_setopt(curl, CURLOPT_WRITEDATA, &buf);\n\n    /* Set mandatory headers */\n    struct curl_slist *headers = NULL;\n    headers = curl_slist_append(headers, \"Content-Type: application/json\");\n    headers = curl_slist_append(headers, \"X-SC-Schema-Version: 2024-06\");\n    headers = curl_slist_append(headers, \"X-SC-Request-ID: demo-client-001\");\n    curl_easy_setopt(curl, CURLOPT_HTTPHEADER, headers);\n\n    /* Perform request */\n    CURLcode rc = curl_easy_perform(curl);\n    if (rc != CURLE_OK) die(\"Gateway request failed\", rc);\n\n    /* HTTP status */\n    long code;\n    curl_easy_getinfo(curl, CURLINFO_RESPONSE_CODE, &code);\n    printf(\"HTTP %ld\\n%s\\n\", code, buf.data);\n\n    /* Cleanup */\n    curl_slist_free_all(headers);\n    free(buf.data);\n    curl_easy_cleanup(curl);\n    return 0;\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 3. <pthread.h>\n#include <signal.h>\n#include <stdatomic.h>\n#include <stdint.h>\n#include <stdbool.h>\n#include <time.h>\n#include <errno.h>\n#include <string.h>\n#include <stdio.h>\n#include <stdlib.h>\n#include <unistd.h>\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/* Constants & Tunables                                                      */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n#define DEFAULT_PORT          8080\n#define MAX_BODY_SIZE         (1024 * 1024) /* 1 MiB  */\n#define TOKEN_BUCKET_RATE     20            /* req / sec  */\n#define TOKEN_BUCKET_CAP      60            /* burst     */\n#define HTTP_THREAD_POOL      8\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/* Utilities                                                                 */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\nstatic uint64_t\nnow_millis(void)\n{\n    struct timespec ts;\n    clock_gettime(CLOCK_REALTIME, &ts);\n    return (uint64_t) ts.tv_sec * 1000ULL + ts.tv_nsec / 1000000ULL;\n}\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/* Configuration                                                             */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\ntypedef struct {\n    uint16_t port;\n    char    *graphql_upstream;      /* e.g. http://graphql-engine:4000/graphql */\n    char    *rest_upstream_prefix;  /* e.g. http://rest-router:7000 */\n} gateway_conf_t;\n\nstatic void\nconf_free(gateway_conf_t *conf)\n{\n    if (!conf) return;\n    free(conf->graphql_upstream);\n    free(conf->rest_upstream_prefix);\n    free(conf);\n}\n\nstatic gateway_conf_t *\nconf_load(const char *path)\n{\n    FILE *fp = fopen(path, \"rb\");\n    if (!fp) {\n        syslog(LOG_ERR, \"cannot open config '%s': %s\", path, strerror(errno));\n        return NULL;\n    }\n\n    fseek(fp, 0, SEEK_END);\n    long len = ftell(fp);\n    rewind(fp);\n\n    char *buf = malloc(len + 1);\n    fread(buf, 1, len, fp);\n    buf[len] = '\\0';\n    fclose(fp);\n\n    cJSON *json = cJSON_Parse(buf);\n    free(buf);\n    if (!json) {\n        syslog(LOG_ERR, \"invalid JSON in config file\");\n        return NULL;\n    }\n\n    gateway_conf_t *cfg = calloc(1, sizeof(*cfg));\n    cfg->port               = (uint16_t) cJSON_GetObjectItemCaseSensitive(json, \"port\")->valueint ?: DEFAULT_PORT;\n    cfg->graphql_upstream   = strdup(cJSON_GetObjectItemCaseSensitive(json, \"graphql_upstream\")->valuestring);\n    cfg->rest_upstream_prefix =\n        strdup(cJSON_GetObjectItemCaseSensitive(json, \"rest_upstream_prefix\")->valuestring);\n\n    cJSON_Delete(json);\n    return cfg;\n}\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/* Token Bucket Rate-Limiting                                                */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\ntypedef struct {\n    uint64_t last_refill_ms;\n    int64_t  tokens;\n    pthread_mutex_t mtx;\n} bucket_t;\n\nstatic bucket_t *\nbucket_create(void)\n{\n    bucket_t *b = calloc(1, sizeof(*b));\n    b->last_refill_ms = now_millis();\n    b->tokens         = TOKEN_BUCKET_CAP;\n    pthread_mutex_init(&b->mtx, NULL);\n    return b;\n}\n\nstatic bool\nbucket_take(bucket_t *b)\n{\n    bool allowed = false;\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&b->mtx);\n\n    uint64_t now = now_millis();\n    uint64_t elapsed = now - b->last_refill_ms;\n\n    if (elapsed >= 1000) {\n        int64_t refill = (elapsed / 1000) * TOKEN_BUCKET_RATE;\n        if (refill > 0) {\n            b->tokens = (b->tokens + refill > TOKEN_BUCKET_CAP)\n                      ? TOKEN_BUCKET_CAP\n                      : b->tokens + refill;\n            b->last_refill_ms = now;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (b->tokens > 0) {\n        b->tokens--;\n        allowed = true;\n    }\n\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&b->mtx);\n    return allowed;\n}\n\n/* Very thin IP→bucket map (open addressing). Not built for huge cardinality,\n * but sufficient for small public endpoints. */\n#define BUCKET_MAP_SZ  1024\ntypedef struct {\n    char     *ip;\n    bucket_t *bucket;\n} bucket_map_slot_t;\n\nstatic bucket_map_slot_t bucket_map[BUCKET_MAP_SZ];\nstatic pthread_mutex_t   bucket_map_mtx = PTHREAD_MUTEX_INITIALIZER;\n\nstatic bucket_t *\nrate_limit_get_bucket(const char *ip)\n{\n    uint32_t h = 0;\n    for (const char *p = ip; *p; ++p)\n        h = (h * 33u) ^ (unsigned)(*p);\n    uint32_t idx = h % BUCKET_MAP_SZ;\n\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&bucket_map_mtx);\n    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < BUCKET_MAP_SZ; ++i) {\n        uint32_t probe = (idx + i) % BUCKET_MAP_SZ;\n\n        if (bucket_map[probe].ip == NULL) {\n            /* create slot */\n            bucket_map[probe].ip     = strdup(ip);\n            bucket_map[probe].bucket = bucket_create();\n            pthread_mutex_unlock(&bucket_map_mtx);\n            return bucket_map[probe].bucket;\n        }\n\n        if (strcmp(bucket_map[probe].ip, ip) == 0) {\n            pthread_mutex_unlock(&bucket_map_mtx);\n            return bucket_map[probe].bucket;\n        }\n    }\n    /* Fallback (evict) */\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&bucket_map_mtx);\n    return bucket_create();\n}\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/* Up-stream Proxy Helpers (libcurl)                                         */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\ntypedef struct {\n    char *ptr;\n    size_t len;\n} dynbuf_t;\n\nstatic size_t\ncurl_write_cb(char *data, size_t size, size_t nmemb, void *userdata)\n{\n    size_t real = size * nmemb;\n    dynbuf_t *db = userdata;\n\n    char *tmp = realloc(db->ptr, db->len + real + 1);\n    if (!tmp) return 0;\n\n    memcpy(tmp + db->len, data, real);\n    db->ptr = tmp;\n    db->len += real;\n    db->ptr[db->len] = '\\0';\n    return real;\n}\n\nstatic int\nproxy_post(const char *url, const char *body, struct MHD_Response **resp, unsigned int *status)\n{\n    CURL *curl = curl_easy_init();\n    if (!curl) return -1;\n\n    dynbuf_t db = { .ptr = NULL, .len = 0 };\n    struct curl_slist *hdrs = NULL;\n    hdrs = curl_slist_append(hdrs, \"Content-Type: application/json\");\n\n    curl_easy_setopt(curl, CURLOPT_URL, url);\n    curl_easy_setopt(curl, CURLOPT_POSTFIELDS, body);\n    curl_easy_setopt(curl, CURLOPT_HTTPHEADER, hdrs);\n    curl_easy_setopt(curl, CURLOPT_WRITEFUNCTION, curl_write_cb);\n    curl_easy_setopt(curl, CURLOPT_WRITEDATA, &db);\n    curl_easy_setopt(curl, CURLOPT_TIMEOUT, 5L);\n\n    CURLcode ret = curl_easy_perform(curl);\n    long http_code = 502;\n    curl_easy_getinfo(curl, CURLINFO_RESPONSE_CODE, &http_code);\n\n    curl_slist_free_all(hdrs);\n    curl_easy_cleanup(curl);\n\n    if (ret != CURLE_OK) {\n        syslog(LOG_ERR, \"curl failed: %s\", curl_easy_strerror(ret));\n        return -1;\n    }\n\n    *status = (unsigned int)http_code;\n    *resp   = MHD_create_response_from_buffer(db.len, db.ptr, MHD_RESPMEM_MUST_FREE);\n    return 0;\n}\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/* Request Context for libmicrohttpd                                         */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\ntypedef struct {\n    char   *body;\n    size_t  body_size;\n} request_ctx_t;\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/* Handler Implementations                                                   */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\nstatic int\nreply_json(struct MHD_Connection *conn, unsigned int status,\n           const char *json)\n{\n    struct MHD_Response *resp =\n        MHD_create_response_from_buffer(strlen(json),\n                                        (void *)json,\n                                        MHD_RESPMEM_PERSISTENT);\n    MHD_add_response_header(resp, \"Content-Type\", \"application/json\");\n    int ret = MHD_queue_response(conn, status, resp);\n    MHD_destroy_response(resp);\n    return ret;\n}\n\nstatic int\nhandle_healthz(struct MHD_Connection *conn)\n{\n    return reply_json(conn, MHD_HTTP_OK, \"{\\\"status\\\":\\\"ok\\\"}\");\n}\n\nstatic int\nhandle_graphql(struct MHD_Connection *conn,\n               request_ctx_t *rctx,\n               const gateway_conf_t *cfg)\n{\n    if (!rctx->body) {\n        return reply_json(conn, MHD_HTTP_BAD_REQUEST,\n                          \"{\\\"error\\\":\\\"empty body\\\"}\");\n    }\n\n    cJSON *json = cJSON_ParseWithLength(rctx->body, rctx->body_size);\n    if (!json) {\n        return reply_json(conn, MHD_HTTP_BAD_REQUEST,\n                          \"{\\\"error\\\":\\\"invalid json\\\"}\");\n    }\n\n    cJSON *query = cJSON_GetObjectItemCaseSensitive(json, \"query\");\n    if (!cJSON_IsString(query) || query->valuestring[0] == '\\0') {\n        cJSON_Delete(json);\n        return reply_json(conn, MHD_HTTP_BAD_REQUEST,\n                          \"{\\\"error\\\":\\\"'query' must be non-empty\\\"}\");\n    }\n    cJSON_Delete(json);\n\n    /* Proxy to upstream */\n    struct MHD_Response *resp;\n    unsigned int status;\n    if (proxy_post(cfg->graphql_upstream, rctx->body, &resp, &status) != 0) {\n        return reply_json(conn, MHD_HTTP_BAD_GATEWAY,\n                          \"{\\\"error\\\":\\\"upstream failure\\\"}\");\n    }\n\n    MHD_add_response_header(resp, \"Content-Type\", \"application/json\");\n    int ret = MHD_queue_response(conn, status, resp);\n    MHD_destroy_response(resp);\n    return ret;\n}\n\nstatic int\nhandle_rest(struct MHD_Connection *conn,\n            const char           *url,\n            const char           *method,\n            request_ctx_t        *rctx,\n            const gateway_conf_t *cfg)\n{\n    /* Compose upstream URL. Simple concatenation. */\n    char *full;\n    if (asprintf(&full, \"%s%s\", cfg->rest_upstream_prefix, url) == -1)\n        return reply_json(conn, MHD_HTTP_INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,\n                          \"{\\\"error\\\":\\\"oom\\\"}\");\n\n    struct MHD_Response *resp = NULL;\n    unsigned int status = 0;\n\n    if (strcasecmp(method, \"GET\") == 0) {\n        /* For brevity, reuse POST path with empty body for GET. */\n        if (proxy_post(full, \"\", &resp, &status) != 0) {\n            free(full);\n            return reply_json(conn, MHD_HTTP_BAD_GATEWAY,\n                              \"{\\\"error\\\":\\\"upstream failure\\\"}\");\n        }\n    } else if (strcasecmp(method, \"POST\") == 0\n               || strcasecmp(method, \"PUT\") == 0\n               || strcasecmp(method,                 \"{\\\"error\\\":\\\"empty body\\\"}\");\n        }\n        if (proxy_post(full, rctx->body, &resp, &status) != 0) {\n            free(full);\n            return reply_json(conn, MHD_HTTP_BAD_GATEWAY,\n                              \"{\\\"error\\\":\\\"upstream failure\\\"}\");\n        }\n    } else {\n        free(full);\n        return reply_json(conn, MHD_HTTP_METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED,\n                          \"{\\\"error\\\":\\\"method not allowed\\\"}\");\n    }\n\n    free(full);\n    MHD_add_response_header(resp, \"Content-Type\", \"application/json\");\n    int ret = MHD_queue_response(conn, status, resp);\n    MHD_destroy_response(resp);\n    return ret;\n}\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/* Dispatcher                                                                */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\nstatic int\naccess_handler_cb(void *cls,\n                  struct MHD_Connection *conn,\n                  const char *url,\n                  const char *method,\n                  const char *version,\n                  const char *upload_data,\n                  size_t      *upload_data_size,\n                  void       **con_cls)\n{\n    (void)version; /* unused */\n\n    gateway_conf_t *cfg = cls;\n    request_ctx_t *rctx = *con_cls;\n\n    /* 1st call -> create per-request ctx */\n    if (!rctx) {\n        rctx = calloc(1, sizeof(*rctx));\n        *con_cls = rctx;\n        return MHD_YES;\n    }\n\n    /* Collect body (potentially chunked) */\n    if (*upload_data_size > 0) {\n        size_t new_size = rctx->body_size + *upload_data_size;\n        if (new_size > MAX_BODY_SIZE) {\n            return reply_json(conn, MHD_HTTP_REQUEST_ENTITY_TOO_LARGE,\n                              \"{\\\"error\\\":\\\"payload too large\\\"}\");\n        }\n        char *tmp = realloc(rctx->body, new_size + 1);\n        if (!tmp) {\n            return reply_json(conn, inet_ntoa(((struct sockaddr_in*)\n                                MHD_get_connection_info(conn,\n                                  MHD_CONNECTION_INFO_CLIENT_ADDRESS)\n        ret = handle_healthz(conn);\n    } else if (strcmp(url, \"/graphql\") == 0) {\n        ret = handle_graphql(conn, rctx, cfg);\n    } else if (strncmp(url, \"/api/\", 5) == 0) {\n        ret = handle_rest(conn, url, method, rctx, cfg);\n    } else {\n        ret = reply_json(conn, MHD_HTTP_NOT_FOUND,\n                         \"{\\\"error\\\":\\\"not found\\\"}\");\n    }\n\n    /* Clean ctx */\n    if (rctx) {\n        free(rctx->body);\n        free(rctx);\n        *con_cls = NULL;\n    }\n    return ret;\n}\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/* Shutdown handling                                                         */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\nstatic volatile sig_atomic_t g_running = 1;\n\nstatic void\nsigint_handler(int sig)\n{\n    (void)sig;\n    g_running = 0;\n}\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/* Main                                                                      */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\nint\nmain(int argc, char **argv)\n{\n    (void)argc; (void)argv;\n\n    openlog(\"syn_canvas_gateway\", LOG_PID | LOG_CONS, LOG_DAEMON);\n\n    const char *conf_path = getenv(\"CONF\");\n    if (!conf_path) conf_path = \"./gateway.conf\";\n\n    gateway_conf_t *cfg = conf_load(conf_path);\n    if (!cfg) {\n        syslog(LOG_ERR, \"failed to load configuration; exiting\");\n        return EXIT_FAILURE;\n    }\n\n    curl_global_init(CURL_GLOBAL_DEFAULT);\n\n    struct MHD_Daemon *d = MHD_start_daemon(\n                                MHD_USE_THREAD_POOL | MHD_USE_TCP_FASTOPEN,\n                                cfg->port,\n                                NULL, NULL,\n                                &access_handler_cb, cfg,\n                                MHD_OPTION_THREAD_POOL_SIZE, HTTP_THREAD_POOL,\n                                MHD_OPTION_END);\n    if (!d) {\n        syslog(LOG_ERR, \"failed to start HTTP daemon on port %u\", cfg->port);\n        conf_free(cfg);\n        return EXIT_FAILURE;\n    }\n\n    syslog(LOG_INFO, \"gateway started on port %u\", cfg->port);\n\n    signal(SIGINT, sigint_handler);\n    signal(SIGTERM, sigint_handler);\n\n    while (g_running) {\n        pause();\n    }\n\n    syslog(LOG_INFO, \"shutting down\");\n    MHD_stop_daemon(d);\n    curl_global_cleanup();\n    conf_free(cfg);\n    closelog();\n    return EXIT_SUCCESS;\n}\n```","numLines":532,"startLine":1,"totalLines":532}}}

The analysis covers component identification, data flow analysis, a phased migration strategy, and risk assessment. The migration is deemed feasible with careful planning, particularly around build system modifications and a parallel deployment strategy to ensure zero-downtime transitions.\n\n---\n\n## 1. Component Identification: Current IPC System\n\n### 1.1 Shared IPC Library (`sc_ipc`)\n\nThe core inter-service communication is built on a custom UNIX domain socket-based IPC library.\n\n| Component | Path | Description |\n|-----------|------|-------------|\n| **Header** | `libs/sc_ipc/include/sc_ipc.h` | Public API definitions, error codes, message types |\n| **Implementation** | `libs/sc_ipc/src/sc_ipc.c` | Socket management, message framing, send/recv operations |\n\n**Key API Functions (from `sc_ipc.h`):**\n\n```c\n// Server-side initialization\nint sc_ipc_server_init(sc_ipc_channel_t *srv, const char *endpoint, size_t backlog);\nint sc_ipc_accept(const sc_ipc_channel_t *srv, sc_ipc_channel_t *out_cli, uint32_t timeout_ms);\n\n// Client-side initialization\nint sc_ipc_client_init(sc_ipc_channel_t *cli, const char *endpoint, uint32_t timeout_ms);\n\n// Message I/O\nint sc_ipc_send_msg(sc_ipc_channel_t *chan, sc_ipc_msg_type_t type, const void *payload,\n                    size_t payload_len, uint64_t correlation_id, uint32_t timeout_ms);\nint sc_ipc_recv_msg(sc_ipc_channel_t *chan, sc_ipc_msg_hdr_t *hdr, void *buf,\n                    size_t buf_size, uint32_t timeout_ms);\n\n// Cleanup\nvoid sc_ipc_close(sc_ipc_channel_t *chan);\n```\n\n**Protocol Characteristics:**\n- Transport: UNIX domain sockets (AF_UNIX)\n- Framing: Length-prefixed (4-byte big-endian header)\n- Maximum payload: 64 KiB (`SC_IPC_MAX_PAYLOAD`)\n- Message types: REQUEST, RESPONSE, EVENT, HEARTBEAT\n- Correlation ID support for request/response matching\n\n### 1.2 API Gateway Client-Side Implementation\n\n| Component | Path | Description |\n|-----------|------|-------------|\n| **Service Client** | `api-gateway/src/services/service_client.c` | HTTP/REST client wrapper using libcurl |\n| **GraphQL Resolvers** | `api-gateway/graphql/resolvers.c` | GraphQL resolver implementations calling backend services |\n| **Gateway Server** | `api-gateway/src/gateway_server.c` | Main gateway entry point with proxying logic |\n\n**Current Communication Pattern:**\nThe API Gateway currently uses **HTTP/REST over libcurl** rather than the raw `sc_ipc` library for inter-service communication. The `service_client.c` (lines 174-198) implements:\n\n```c\nint sc_service_client_call(sc_service_client_t *cli,\n                           sc_service_endp_t    endpoint,\n                           const char          *method,\n                           const char          *path,\n                           const char          *request_json,\n                           cJSON              **response_json,\n                           long                *http_status);\n```\n\n**Service Endpoint Configuration (from `service_client.c`, lines 41-44):**\n- `SC_PALETTE_SERVICE_URL` - Palette service base URL\n- `SC_TEXTURE_SERVICE_URL` - Texture service base URL\n- `SC_AUDIO_SERVICE_URL` - Audio service base URL\n- `SC_NARRATIVE_SERVICE_URL` - Narrative service base URL\n\n### 1.3 Backend Microservice Implementations\n\n| Service | Main Entry Point | Handler | Port |\n|---------|-----------------|---------|------|\n| **Texture Service** | `services/texture-service/src/main.c` | `services/texture-service/src/texture_handler.c` | 7002 |\n| **Palette Service** | `services/palette-service/src/main.c` | N/A (inline) | 7001 |\n| **Audio Service** | `services/audio-service/src/main.c` | TBD | 7003 |\n| **Narrative Service** | `services/narrative-service/src/main.c` | TBD | 7004 |\n\n**Current Backend HTTP Server Implementation:**\nAll microservices use **libmicrohttpd** for HTTP serving. From `texture-service/src/main.c` (lines 415-421):\n\n```c\nsvc.daemon = MHD_start_daemon(MHD_USE_AUTO | MHD_USE_INTERNAL_POLLING_THREAD,\n                              svc.cfg.port,\n                              NULL, NULL,\n                              request_router, &svc,\n                              MHD_OPTION_CONNECTION_TIMEOUT, (unsigned int)10,\n                              MHD_OPTION_END);\n```\n\n### 1.4 Common Libraries\n\n| Library | Path | Purpose |\n|---------|------|---------|\n| **sc_common** | `libs/sc_common/` | Shared configuration, logging, error handling |\n| **sc_config.h** | `libs/sc_common/include/sc_config.h` | Runtime configuration structures |\n| **sc_logging.h** | `libs/sc_common/include/sc_logging.h` | Structured logging facade |\n| **sc_errors.h** | `libs/sc_common/include/sc_errors.h` | Error code definitions |\n\n### 1.5 Build System Files\n\n| Component | Path | Modifications Required |\n|-----------|------|----------------------|\n| **Root CMake** | `CMakeLists.txt` | Add gRPC/Protobuf dependencies |\n| **Gateway CMake** | `api-gateway/CMakeLists.txt` | Link gRPC libraries, add proto compilation |\n| **Texture Service CMake** | `services/texture-service/CMakeLists.txt` | Add gRPC server support |\n\n---\n\n## 2. Data Flow Analysis\n\n### 2.1 Current Request Flow (Based on `data_flow.puml`)\n\nThe PlantUML diagram (`docs/architecture/data_flow.puml`) illustrates the following synchronous request path:\n\n```\nClient → API Gateway → Rate Limiter → Redis Cache → Request Validator → GraphQL Resolver\n                                                                              ↓\n                                                                    [Texture Service]\n                                                                              ↓\n                                                                    PostgreSQL/Redis\n                                                                              ↓\n                                                                    Response ← Client\n```\n\n### 2.2 Detailed Request Flow: API Gateway to Texture Service\n\n#### Step 1: Client Request Arrival\n**File:** `api-gateway/src/gateway_server.c` (lines 390-468)\n\n```c\nstatic int access_handler_cb(void *cls, struct MHD_Connection *conn,\n                             const char *url, const char *method, ...)\n```\n\n1. Request received by libmicrohttpd\n2. Rate limiting check via token bucket (`rate_limit_get_bucket()`)\n3. URL routing to appropriate handler\n\n#### Step 2: GraphQL Resolution\n**File:** `api-gateway/graphql/resolvers.c` (lines 184-210)\n\n```c\nstatic json_t *resolve_palette(const json_t *args, void *user_data, GQLError **gql_err)\n{\n    // Extract arguments\n    const char *id = json_string_value(json_object_get(args, \"id\"));\n\n    // Rate limit check\n    if (rate_limiter_acquire(ctx->limiter) != 0) { ... }\n\n    // Build upstream URL\n    char url[512];\n    snprintf(url, sizeof(url), \"%s/palettes/%s\", PALETTE_SERVICE_BASE_URL, id);\n\n    // Call upstream service via HTTP\n    json_t *payload = http_json_get(url, ctx->log, gql_err);\n}\n```\n\n#### Step 3: Service Client Call\n**File:** `api-gateway/src/services/service_client.c` (lines 247-330)\n\n```c\nstatic int perform_request(sc_service_client_t *client, const char *url,\n                           const char *method, const char *json_body,\n                           long *http_status, cJSON **out_body)\n{\n    // Uses libcurl for HTTP communication\n    curl_easy_setopt(h, CURLOPT_URL, url);\n    curl_easy_setopt(h, CURLOPT_HTTPHEADER, hdrs);\n    res = curl_easy_perform(h);\n}\n```\n\n**Key Data Structures:**\n- `sc_service_client_t` - Client state with per-service URLs, metrics\n- `response_buf_t` - Dynamic response buffer\n- Request/response serialization via **cJSON**\n\n#### Step 4: Texture Service Processing\n**File:** `services/texture-service/src/main.c` (lines 347-378)\n\n```c\nstatic enum MHD_Result request_router(void *cls, struct MHD_Connection *connection,\n                                      const char *url, const char *method, ...)\n{\n    if (strcmp(url, \"/healthz\") == 0) {\n        return handle_healthz(connection);\n    }\n    else if (strcmp(url, \"/v1/noise\") == 0) {\n        return handle_noise(svc, connection);\n    }\n}\n```\n\n**Handler Processing:**\n**File:** `services/texture-service/src/texture_handler.c` (lines 132-194)\n\n```c\nTexture *texture_handler_create(TextureHandler *handler, const TextureInput *input,\n                                TextureError *err)\n{\n    // Validation\n    if (!_validate_texture_input(input, err)) return NULL;\n\n    // Build domain object\n    tx.id = _generate_uuid();\n\n    // Repository persistence\n    bool persist_ok = repository_save_texture(state->repo, &tx, err);\n\n    // Metrics update\n    if (state->metrics) metrics_counter_inc(state->metrics, \"texture_created_total\", 1);\n\n    return texture_clone(&tx, err);\n}\n```\n\n#### Step 5: Response Path\n\n1. Handler returns JSON response via `MHD_create_response_from_buffer()`\n2. Response flows back through libmicrohttpd\n3. Gateway receives HTTP response via libcurl\n4. Gateway parses JSON via `cJSON_Parse()`\n5. GraphQL resolver returns payload to client\n\n---\n\n## 3. Migration Strategy Proposal\n\n### 3.1 Overview: Phased Migration Approach\n\nThe migration will follow a **strangler fig pattern** with parallel systems operating simultaneously. This ensures zero downtime and allows for gradual rollout with easy rollback capability.\n\n```\nPhase 1: Infrastructure Setup (gRPC dependencies, proto definitions)\n    ↓\nPhase 2: Texture Service Dual-Protocol Support (gRPC + HTTP)\n    ↓\nPhase 3: API Gateway gRPC Client Integration (Feature Flag)\n    ↓\nPhase 4: Traffic Migration (Gradual rollout)\n    ↓\nPhase 5: Legacy HTTP Deprecation & Removal\n```\n\n### 3.2 Phase 1: Infrastructure Setup\n\n#### 3.2.1 Protocol Buffer Definition\n\n**New File:** `protos/texture.proto`\n\n```protobuf\nsyntax = \"proto3\";\n\npackage synestheticcanvas.texture.v1;\n\noption c_enable_arenas = true;\n\nservice TextureService {\n    rpc CreateTexture(CreateTextureRequest) returns (CreateTextureResponse);\n    rpc GetTexture(GetTextureRequest) returns (GetTextureResponse);\n    rpc ListTextures(ListTexturesRequest) returns (ListTexturesResponse);\n    rpc DeleteTexture(DeleteTextureRequest) returns (DeleteTextureResponse);\n    rpc GetTextureRaw(GetTextureRawRequest) returns (stream TextureChunk);\n}\n\nmessage CreateTextureRequest {\n    string name = 1;\n    string color_profile = 2;\n    uint32 width = 3;\n    uint32 height = 4;\n    float frequency = 5;\n}\n\nmessage CreateTextureResponse {\n    string id = 1;\n    uint32 width = 2;\n    uint32 height = 3;\n    string created_at = 4;\n}\n\nmessage GetTextureRequest {\n    string id = 1;\n}\n\nmessage GetTextureResponse {\n    string id = 1;\n    string name = 2;\n    string color_profile = 3;\n    uint32 width = 4;\n    uint32 height = 5;\n    string created_at = 6;\n}\n\nmessage ListTexturesRequest {\n    uint32 page_size = 1;\n    string page_token = 2;\n}\n\nmessage ListTexturesResponse {\n    repeated GetTextureResponse textures = 1;\n    string next_page_token = 2;\n    uint32 total_count = 3;\n}\n\nmessage DeleteTextureRequest {\n    string id = 1;\n}\n\nmessage DeleteTextureResponse {\n    bool success = 1;\n}\n\nmessage GetTextureRawRequest {\n    string id = 1;\n}\n\nmessage TextureChunk {\n    bytes data = 1;\n    uint64 offset = 2;\n    uint64 total_size = 3;\n}\n```\n\n#### 3.2.2 Root CMakeLists.txt Modifications\n\n**File:** `CMakeLists.txt` (Add after line 127)\n\n```cmake\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n#  gRPC and Protocol Buffers Support\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\noption(SC_ENABLE_GRPC \"Enable gRPC support for inter-service communication\" OFF)\n\nif(SC_ENABLE_GRPC)\n    find_package(Protobuf REQUIRED)\n    find_package(gRPC REQUIRED)\n\n    # Locate grpc_cpp_plugin for code generation\n    find_program(GRPC_CPP_PLUGIN grpc_cpp_plugin REQUIRED)\n\n    # Proto compilation function\n    function(add_proto_library TARGET_NAME PROTO_FILE)\n        get_filename_component(PROTO_PATH ${PROTO_FILE} DIRECTORY)\n        get_filename_component(PROTO_NAME ${PROTO_FILE} NAME_WE)\n\n        set(PROTO_SRCS \"${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/${PROTO_NAME}.pb.c\")\n        set(PROTO_HDRS \"${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/${PROTO_NAME}.pb.h\")\n        set(GRPC_SRCS \"${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/${PROTO_NAME}.grpc.pb.c\")\n        set(GRPC_HDRS \"${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/${PROTO_NAME}.grpc.pb.h\")\n\n        add_custom_command(\n            OUTPUT ${PROTO_SRCS} ${PROTO_HDRS} ${GRPC_SRCS} ${GRPC_HDRS}\n            COMMAND protobuf::protoc\n            ARGS --proto_path=${PROTO_PATH}\n                 --c_out=${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}\n                 --grpc_out=${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}\n                 --plugin=protoc-gen-grpc=${GRPC_CPP_PLUGIN}\n                 ${PROTO_FILE}\n            DEPENDS ${PROTO_FILE}\n        )\n\n        add_library(${TARGET_NAME} STATIC ${PROTO_SRCS} ${GRPC_SRCS})\n        target_include_directories(${TARGET_NAME} PUBLIC ${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR})\n        target_link_libraries(${TARGET_NAME} PUBLIC protobuf::libprotobuf-c gRPC::grpc)\n    endfunction()\n\n    # Compile proto files\n    add_proto_library(texture_proto ${PROJECT_SOURCE_DIR}/protos/texture.proto)\nendif()\n```\n\n### 3.3 Phase 2: Texture Service Dual-Protocol Support\n\nThe texture service will be modified to listen on **two ports simultaneously**: one for the existing HTTP/REST API and one for gRPC.\n\n#### 3.3.1 Texture Service CMakeLists.txt Modifications\n\n**File:** `services/texture-service/CMakeLists.txt` (Add after line 157)\n\n```cmake\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n#  gRPC Server Support (Optional)\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\noption(TEXTURESVC_ENABLE_GRPC \"Enable gRPC server endpoint\" OFF)\n\nif(TEXTURESVC_ENABLE_GRPC)\n    if(NOT TARGET texture_proto)\n        message(FATAL_ERROR \"texture_proto target not found. Build with -DSC_ENABLE_GRPC=ON\")\n    endif()\n\n    target_sources(texture_service PRIVATE\n        src/grpc_server.c\n        src/grpc_handlers.c\n    )\n\n    target_link_libraries(texture_service\n        PRIVATE\n            texture_proto\n            gRPC::grpc\n            gRPC::grpc++\n    )\n\n    target_compile_definitions(texture_service PRIVATE ENABLE_GRPC_SERVER=1)\nendif()\n```\n\n#### 3.3.2 Dual-Protocol Main Entry Point\n\n**New File:** `services/texture-service/src/grpc_server.c`\n\n```c\n/**\n * gRPC Server implementation for Texture Service\n * Runs alongside HTTP server for parallel protocol support\n */\n\n#include <grpc/grpc.h>\n#include <grpc/support/log.h>\n#include \"texture.grpc.pb-c.h\"\n#include \"texture_handler.h\"\n\n#define GRPC_DEFAULT_PORT 17002  // gRPC on separate port\n\ntypedef struct {\n    grpc_server *server;\n    grpc_completion_queue *cq;\n    TextureHandler *handler;\n    volatile bool running;\n} grpc_texture_server_t;\n\n// gRPC service method implementations\nstatic void handle_create_texture(grpc_texture_server_t *srv,\n                                  const CreateTextureRequest *req,\n                                  CreateTextureResponse *resp) {\n    TextureInput input = {\n        .name = req->name,\n        .color_profile = req->color_profile,\n        .width = req->width,\n        .height = req->height\n    };\n\n    TextureError err = {0};\n    Texture *tx = texture_handler_create(srv->handler, &input, &err);\n\n    if (tx) {\n        resp->id = strdup(tx->id);\n        resp->width = tx->width;\n        resp->height = tx->height;\n        // Format timestamp\n        char ts[32];\n        strftime(ts, sizeof(ts), \"%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ\", gmtime(&tx->created_at));\n        resp->created_at = strdup(ts);\n        texture_free(tx);\n    }\n}\n\ngrpc_texture_server_t *grpc_texture_server_create(TextureHandler *handler, uint16_t port);\nvoid grpc_texture_server_serve(grpc_texture_server_t *srv);\nvoid grpc_texture_server_stop(grpc_texture_server_t *srv);\nvoid grpc_texture_server_destroy(grpc_texture_server_t *srv);\n```\n\n#### 3.3.3 Modified main.c for Dual-Protocol\n\n**File:** `services/texture-service/src/main.c` (Modifications)\n\n```c\n#ifdef ENABLE_GRPC_SERVER\n#include \"grpc_server.h\"\n#include <pthread.h>\n\nstatic pthread_t g_grpc_thread;\nstatic grpc_texture_server_t *g_grpc_server = NULL;\n\nstatic void *grpc_server_thread(void *arg) {\n    grpc_texture_server_t *srv = (grpc_texture_server_t *)arg;\n    grpc_texture_server_serve(srv);\n    return NULL;\n}\n#endif\n\nint main(void)\n{\n    ServiceCtx svc;\n    config_set_defaults(&svc.cfg);\n\n    // ... existing HTTP setup ...\n\n#ifdef ENABLE_GRPC_SERVER\n    // Start gRPC server on separate port\n    uint16_t grpc_port = (uint16_t)sc_u16_from_env(\"TEXTURE_GRPC_PORT\", 17002);\n    g_grpc_server = grpc_texture_server_create(svc.handler, grpc_port);\n    if (g_grpc_server) {\n        pthread_create(&g_grpc_thread, NULL, grpc_server_thread, g_grpc_server);\n        log_msg(LOG_INFO, \"gRPC server started on port %u\", grpc_port);\n    }\n#endif\n\n    // Start HTTP daemon (existing code)\n    svc.daemon = MHD_start_daemon(...);\n\n    while (!g_shutdown)\n        sleep(1);\n\n#ifdef ENABLE_GRPC_SERVER\n    if (g_grpc_server) {\n        grpc_texture_server_stop(g_grpc_server);\n        pthread_join(g_grpc_thread, NULL);\n        grpc_texture_server_destroy(g_grpc_server);\n    }\n#endif\n\n    MHD_stop_daemon(svc.daemon);\n    return EXIT_SUCCESS;\n}\n```\n\n### 3.4 Phase 3: API Gateway gRPC Client Integration\n\n#### 3.4.1 Gateway CMakeLists.txt Modifications\n\n**File:** `api-gateway/CMakeLists.txt` (Add after line 147)\n\n```cmake\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n#  gRPC Client Support\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\noption(GATEWAY_ENABLE_GRPC \"Enable gRPC client for upstream services\" OFF)\n\nif(GATEWAY_ENABLE_GRPC)\n    if(NOT TARGET texture_proto)\n        message(FATAL_ERROR \"texture_proto target not found. Build with -DSC_ENABLE_GRPC=ON\")\n    endif()\n\n    target_sources(api_gateway PRIVATE\n        src/services/grpc_client.c\n    )\n\n    target_link_libraries(api_gateway\n        PRIVATE\n            texture_proto\n            gRPC::grpc\n    )\n\n    target_compile_definitions(api_gateway PRIVATE ENABLE_GRPC_CLIENT=1)\nendif()\n```\n\n#### 3.4.2 Feature Flag for Protocol Selection\n\n**File:** `api-gateway/src/services/service_client.c` (Modifications)\n\n```c\n// Add to sc_service_client_t structure\nstruct sc_service_client\n{\n    // ... existing fields ...\n\n#ifdef ENABLE_GRPC_CLIENT\n    /* gRPC channels */\n    grpc_channel *texture_grpc_channel;\n    bool use_grpc_texture;  // Feature flag\n#endif\n};\n\n// Modified initialization\nsc_service_client_t *sc_service_client_new(void)\n{\n    // ... existing code ...\n\n#ifdef ENABLE_GRPC_CLIENT\n    // Check feature flag\n    cli->use_grpc_texture = sc_bool_from_env(\"SC_USE_GRPC_TEXTURE\", false);\n\n    if (cli->use_grpc_texture) {\n        const char *grpc_addr = getenv(\"SC_TEXTURE_GRPC_URL\") ?: \"texture-service:17002\";\n        cli->texture_grpc_channel = grpc_insecure_channel_create(grpc_addr, NULL, NULL);\n        LOG_INF(\"gRPC enabled for texture service: %s\", grpc_addr);\n    }\n#endif\n\n    return cli;\n}\n\n// Modified call function\nint sc_service_client_call(sc_service_client_t *cli,\n                           sc_service_endp_t    endpoint,\n                           const char          *method,\n                           const char          *path,\n                           const char          *request_json,\n                           cJSON              **response_json,\n                           long                *http_status)\n{\n#ifdef ENABLE_GRPC_CLIENT\n    if (endpoint == SC_EP_TEXTURE && cli->use_grpc_texture) {\n        return sc_service_client_call_grpc(cli, path, request_json,\n                                           response_json, http_status);\n    }\n#endif\n\n    // Fall back to HTTP\n    return sc_service_client_call_http(cli, endpoint, method, path,\n                                       request_json, response_json, http_status);\n}\n```\n\n### 3.5 Phase 4: Traffic Migration Strategy\n\n#### 3.5.1 Deployment Configuration\n\n**Modified docker-compose.yml:**\n\n```yaml\ntexture_service:\n  environment:\n    <<: *default-env\n    SERVICE_PORT: 7002\n    TEXTURE_GRPC_PORT: 17002  # New gRPC port\n  ports:\n    - \"7002:7002\"    # HTTP (existing)\n    - \"17002:17002\"  # gRPC (new)\n\napi_gateway:\n  environment:\n    <<: *default-env\n    SC_USE_GRPC_TEXTURE: \"false\"  # Start with HTTP\n    SC_TEXTURE_GRPC_URL: \"texture_service:17002\"\n```\n\n#### 3.5.2 Gradual Rollout Process\n\n1. **Week 1:** Deploy texture service with dual-protocol support\n   - Monitor gRPC health endpoint\n   - Verify no performance degradation\n\n2. **Week 2:** Enable gRPC for internal testing\n   ```bash\n   export SC_USE_GRPC_TEXTURE=true  # On test gateway instances\n   ```\n\n3. **Week 3:** Canary deployment (5% traffic)\n   - Use load balancer weighted routing\n   - Monitor error rates and latency\n\n4. **Week 4:** Gradual increase (25% → 50% → 100%)\n\n5. **Week 5:** Deprecate HTTP endpoints (keep for rollback)\n\n### 3.6 Phase 5: Legacy HTTP Deprecation\n\nAfter successful migration:\n\n1. Remove HTTP endpoints from texture service\n2. Remove HTTP client code from gateway\n3. Update CMakeLists.txt to make gRPC the default\n4. Remove feature flags\n\n---\n\n## 4. Risk Assessment\n\n### 4.1 Risk 1: Build System Complexity\n\n**Severity:** HIGH\n**Likelihood:** HIGH\n\n**Description:**\nAdding gRPC to a pure C project introduces significant build system complexity. gRPC's C implementation requires:\n- Protocol Buffers compiler and runtime (`protobuf-c`)\n- gRPC C core library (`grpc`)\n- C++ toolchain (gRPC core has C++ dependencies)\n\n**Evidence:**\nCurrent CMakeLists.txt uses pure C with `-std=c17` standard (see `api-gateway/CMakeLists.txt`, line 66). gRPC-C requires linking against C++ runtime.\n\n**Mitigation:**\n- Use `grpc` C-core library instead of `grpc++`\n- Consider using `protobuf-c` for pure C protobuf support\n- Provide comprehensive Docker build images with pre-installed dependencies\n- Implement FetchContent fallbacks for missing system packages\n\n### 4.2 Risk 2: Performance Regression\n\n**Severity:** MEDIUM\n**Likelihood:** MEDIUM\n\n**Description:**\nThe current custom IPC uses UNIX domain sockets with minimal framing overhead (4-byte length prefix). gRPC introduces HTTP/2 framing overhead, protobuf serialization, and TLS handshakes (if enabled).\n\n**Evidence:**\nFrom `libs/sc_ipc/src/sc_ipc.c` (line 315-340), the current protocol uses simple `writev()` syscalls with minimal overhead.\n\n**Performance Characteristics Comparison:**\n\n| Aspect | Custom IPC | gRPC (HTTP/2) |\n|--------|-----------|---------------|\n| Framing overhead | 4 bytes | ~20-50 bytes |\n| Serialization | JSON/raw | Protobuf (binary) |\n| Connection setup | None (UNIX socket) | TCP + HTTP/2 handshake |\n| Latency (typical) | <1ms | 1-5ms |\n| Throughput | Higher | Lower (due to framing) |\n\n**Mitigation:**\n- Enable gRPC keep-alive to reduce connection overhead\n- Use streaming for large payloads (texture data)\n- Consider Unix Domain Socket transport for gRPC (if same host)\n- Benchmark before and after migration\n\n### 4.3 Risk 3: Operational Complexity During Transition\n\n**Severity:** MEDIUM\n**Likelihood:** HIGH\n\n**Description:**\nRunning a hybrid system with both HTTP and gRPC endpoints increases operational burden:\n- Doubled port exposure and monitoring\n- Complex debugging (two protocols)\n- Inconsistent behavior during transition\n\n**Evidence:**\nDocker-compose configuration shows services already expose multiple ports (see `docker-compose.yml`, lines 59-61).\n\n**Mitigation:**\n- Unified observability (OpenTelemetry supports both HTTP and gRPC)\n- Single health check endpoint covering both protocols\n- Clear runbooks for protocol-specific troubleshooting\n- Automated testing for both code paths\n\n### 4.4 Risk 4: Serialization Compatibility\n\n**Severity:** MEDIUM\n**Likelihood:** LOW\n\n**Description:**\nThe current system uses JSON for all payloads. Migration to Protobuf requires careful schema definition to maintain backward compatibility.\n\n**Evidence:**\nFrom `api-gateway/graphql/resolvers.c` (line 125), responses are parsed with `json_loads()`. From `services/texture-service/src/texture_handler.c` (line 373-394), JSON serialization is used throughout.\n\n**Mitigation:**\n- Define protobuf schemas that map 1:1 with existing JSON\n- Add `json_name` options for field name compatibility\n- Maintain JSON gateway for external consumers\n- Version protobuf schemas from the start\n\n### 4.5 Risk 5: Debugging and Observability\n\n**Severity:** LOW\n**Likelihood:** MEDIUM\n\n**Description:**\nJSON payloads are human-readable and easy to debug. Protobuf binary encoding requires additional tooling.\n\n**Evidence:**\nCurrent logging in `service_client.c` (lines 337-354) outputs JSON directly to stderr.\n\n**Mitigation:**\n- Use gRPC reflection service for introspection\n- Implement protobuf-to-JSON logging adapter\n- Use `grpcurl` or similar tools for debugging\n- Enable gRPC verbose logging during development\n\n---\n\n## 5. New gRPC-Based Communication Flow\n\n### 5.1 Request Flow Diagram\n\n```\n                              ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n                              │                    API Gateway                          │\n                              │                                                         │\nClient ──HTTP──► ┌────────────┼────────────┐     ┌─────────────┐     ┌───────────────┐ │\n     Request     │Rate Limiter│            │     │ GraphQL     │     │ gRPC Client   │ │\n                 └────────────┼────────────┘     │ Resolver    │────►│ (Texture)     │─┼──┐\n                              │                  └─────────────┘     └───────────────┘ │  │\n                              └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘  │\n                                                                                           │\n                                                                         gRPC over HTTP/2  │\n                                                                                           │\n                              ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐  │\n                              │                  Texture Service                        │  │\n                              │                                                         │  │\n                              │  ┌───────────────┐     ┌───────────────┐               │  │\n                         ─────┼─►│ gRPC Server   │────►│ Texture       │               │◄─┘\n                              │  │ (port 17002)  │     │ Handler       │               │\n                              │  └───────────────┘     └───────────────┘               │\n                              │                              │                         │\n                              │                              ▼                         │\n                              │                       ┌────────────┐                   │\n                              │                       │ Repository │                   │\n                              │                       │ (Postgres) │                   │\n                              │                       └────────────┘                   │\n                              └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n### 5.2 Sequence Diagram\n\n```\nClient          Gateway           gRPC Client        Texture Service\n  │                │                   │                    │\n  │─HTTP Request──►│                   │                    │\n  │                │                   │                    │\n  │                │──validate─────────│                    │\n  │                │                   │                    │\n  │                │──CreateTextureRequest (protobuf)──────►│\n  │                │                   │                    │\n  │                │                   │         ┌──────────┤\n  │                │                   │         │  Handler │\n  │                │                   │         │processing│\n  │                │                   │         └──────────┤\n  │                │                   │                    │\n  │                │◄──CreateTextureResponse (protobuf)─────│\n  │                │                   │                    │\n  │◄─HTTP Response─│                   │                    │\n  │                │                   │                    │\n```\n\n---\n\n## 6. Conclusion\n\nThe migration from custom IPC to gRPC is technically feasible and will provide significant benefits:\n\n**Benefits:**\n- Standardized interface definitions (Protocol Buffers)\n- Language-agnostic service contracts\n- Built-in streaming support for large payloads\n- Rich ecosystem (reflection, health checks, load balancing)\n- Better tooling for debugging and testing\n\n**Recommended Approach:**\n1. Start with the texture service as a pilot\n2. Use parallel protocol deployment (no big-bang cutover)\n3. Feature flag-controlled gradual rollout\n4. Maintain HTTP fallback for rollback capability\n\n**Timeline Estimate:**\n- Phase 1 (Infrastructure): 2-3 weeks\n- Phase 2 (Texture Service): 2-3 weeks\n- Phase 3 (Gateway Client): 2 weeks\n- Phase 4 (Migration): 4-6 weeks\n- Phase 5 (Cleanup): 1-2 weeks\n\n**Total:** 11-16 weeks for complete migration\n\n---\n\n## 7. The analysis covers component identification, data flow analysis, a phased migration strategy, and risk assessment. The migration is deemed feasible with careful planning, particularly around build system modifications and a parallel deployment strategy to ensure zero-downtime transitions.\n\n---\n\n## 1. Component Identification: Current IPC System\n\n### 1.1 Shared IPC Library (`sc_ipc`)\n\nThe core inter-service communication is built on a custom UNIX domain socket-based IPC library.\n\n| Component | Path | Description |\n|-----------|------|-------------|\n| **Header** | `libs/sc_ipc/include/sc_ipc.h` | Public API definitions, error codes, message types |\n| **Implementation** | `libs/sc_ipc/src/sc_ipc.c` | Socket management, message framing, send/recv operations |\n\n**Key API Functions (from `sc_ipc.h`):**\n\n```c\n// Server-side initialization\nint sc_ipc_server_init(sc_ipc_channel_t *srv, const char *endpoint, size_t backlog);\nint sc_ipc_accept(const sc_ipc_channel_t *srv, sc_ipc_channel_t *out_cli, uint32_t timeout_ms);\n\n// Client-side initialization\nint sc_ipc_client_init(sc_ipc_channel_t *cli, const char *endpoint, uint32_t timeout_ms);\n\n// Message I/O\nint sc_ipc_send_msg(sc_ipc_channel_t *chan, sc_ipc_msg_type_t type, const void *payload,\n                    size_t payload_len, uint64_t correlation_id, uint32_t timeout_ms);\nint sc_ipc_recv_msg(sc_ipc_channel_t *chan, sc_ipc_msg_hdr_t *hdr, void *buf,\n                    size_t buf_size, uint32_t timeout_ms);\n\n// Cleanup\nvoid sc_ipc_close(sc_ipc_channel_t *chan);\n```\n\n**Protocol Characteristics:**\n- Transport: UNIX domain sockets (AF_UNIX)\n- Framing: Length-prefixed (4-byte big-endian header)\n- Maximum payload: 64 KiB (`SC_IPC_MAX_PAYLOAD`)\n- Message types: REQUEST, RESPONSE, EVENT, HEARTBEAT\n- Correlation ID support for request/response matching\n\n### 1.2 API Gateway Client-Side Implementation\n\n| Component | Path | Description |\n|-----------|------|-------------|\n| **Service Client** | `api-gateway/src/services/service_client.c` | HTTP/REST client wrapper using libcurl |\n| **GraphQL Resolvers** | `api-gateway/graphql/resolvers.c` | GraphQL resolver implementations calling backend services |\n| **Gateway Server** | `api-gateway/src/gateway_server.c` | Main gateway entry point with proxying logic |\n\n**Current Communication Pattern:**\nThe API Gateway currently uses **HTTP/REST over libcurl** rather than the raw `sc_ipc` library for inter-service communication. The `service_client.c` (lines 174-198) implements:\n\n```c\nint sc_service_client_call(sc_service_client_t *cli,\n                           sc_service_endp_t    endpoint,\n                           const char          *method,\n                           const char          *path,\n                           const char          *request_json,\n                           cJSON              **response_json,\n                           long                *http_status);\n```\n\n**Service Endpoint Configuration (from `service_client.c`, lines 41-44):**\n- `SC_PALETTE_SERVICE_URL` - Palette service base URL\n- `SC_TEXTURE_SERVICE_URL` - Texture service base URL\n- `SC_AUDIO_SERVICE_URL` - Audio service base URL\n- `SC_NARRATIVE_SERVICE_URL` - Narrative service base URL\n\n### 1.3 Backend Microservice Implementations\n\n| Service | Main Entry Point | Handler | Port |\n|---------|-----------------|---------|------|\n| **Texture Service** | `services/texture-service/src/main.c` | `services/texture-service/src/texture_handler.c` | 7002 |\n| **Palette Service** | `services/palette-service/src/main.c` | N/A (inline) | 7001 |\n| **Audio Service** | `services/audio-service/src/main.c` | TBD | 7003 |\n| **Narrative Service** | `services/narrative-service/src/main.c` | TBD | 7004 |\n\n**Current Backend HTTP Server Implementation:**\nAll microservices use **libmicrohttpd** for HTTP serving. From `texture-service/src/main.c` (lines 415-421):\n\n```c\nsvc.daemon = MHD_start_daemon(MHD_USE_AUTO | MHD_USE_INTERNAL_POLLING_THREAD,\n                              svc.cfg.port,\n                              NULL, NULL,\n                              request_router, &svc,\n                              MHD_OPTION_CONNECTION_TIMEOUT, (unsigned int)10,\n                              MHD_OPTION_END);\n```\n\n### 1.4 Common Libraries\n\n| Library | Path | Purpose |\n|---------|------|---------|\n| **sc_common** | `libs/sc_common/` | Shared configuration, logging, error handling |\n| **sc_config.h** | `libs/sc_common/include/sc_config.h` | Runtime configuration structures |\n| **sc_logging.h** | `libs/sc_common/include/sc_logging.h` | Structured logging facade |\n| **sc_errors.h** | `libs/sc_common/include/sc_errors.h` | Error code definitions |\n\n### 1.5 Build System Files\n\n| Component | Path | Modifications Required |\n|-----------|------|----------------------|\n| **Root CMake** | `CMakeLists.txt` | Add gRPC/Protobuf dependencies |\n| **Gateway CMake** | `api-gateway/CMakeLists.txt` | Link gRPC libraries, add proto compilation |\n| **Texture Service CMake** | `services/texture-service/CMakeLists.txt` | Add gRPC server support |\n\n---\n\n## 2. Data Flow Analysis\n\n### 2.1 Current Request Flow (Based on `data_flow.puml`)\n\nThe PlantUML diagram (`docs/architecture/data_flow.puml`) illustrates the following synchronous request path:\n\n```\nClient → API Gateway → Rate Limiter → Redis Cache → Request Validator → GraphQL Resolver\n                                                                              ↓\n                                                                    [Texture Service]\n                                                                              ↓\n                                                                    PostgreSQL/Redis\n                                                                              ↓\n                                                                    Response ← Client\n```\n\n### 2.2 Detailed Request Flow: API Gateway to Texture Service\n\n#### Step 1: Client Request Arrival\n**File:** `api-gateway/src/gateway_server.c` (lines 390-468)\n\n```c\nstatic int access_handler_cb(void *cls, struct MHD_Connection *conn,\n                             const char *url, const char *method, ...)\n```\n\n1. Request received by libmicrohttpd\n2. Rate limiting check via token bucket (`rate_limit_get_bucket()`)\n3. URL routing to appropriate handler\n\n#### Step 2: GraphQL Resolution\n**File:** `api-gateway/graphql/resolvers.c` (lines 184-210)\n\n```c\nstatic json_t *resolve_palette(const json_t *args, void *user_data, GQLError **gql_err)\n{\n    // Extract arguments\n    const char *id = json_string_value(json_object_get(args, \"id\"));\n\n    // Rate limit check\n    if (rate_limiter_acquire(ctx->limiter) != 0) { ... }\n\n    // Build upstream URL\n    char url[512];\n    snprintf(url, sizeof(url), \"%s/palettes/%s\", PALETTE_SERVICE_BASE_URL, id);\n\n    // Call upstream service via HTTP\n    json_t *payload = http_json_get(url, ctx->log, gql_err);\n}\n```\n\n#### Step 3: Service Client Call\n**File:** `api-gateway/src/services/service_client.c` (lines 247-330)\n\n```c\nstatic int perform_request(sc_service_client_t *client, const char *url,\n                           const char *method, const char *json_body,\n                           long *http_status, cJSON **out_body)\n{\n    // Uses libcurl for HTTP communication\n    curl_easy_setopt(h, CURLOPT_URL, url);\n    curl_easy_setopt(h, CURLOPT_HTTPHEADER, hdrs);\n    res = curl_easy_perform(h);\n}\n```\n\n**Key Data Structures:**\n- `sc_service_client_t` - Client state with per-service URLs, metrics\n- `response_buf_t` - Dynamic response buffer\n- Request/response serialization via **cJSON**\n\n#### Step 4: Texture Service Processing\n**File:** `services/texture-service/src/main.c` (lines 347-378)\n\n```c\nstatic enum MHD_Result request_router(void *cls, struct MHD_Connection *connection,\n                                      const char *url, const char *method, ...)\n{\n    if (strcmp(url, \"/healthz\") == 0) {\n        return handle_healthz(connection);\n    }\n    else if (strcmp(url, \"/v1/noise\") == 0) {\n        return handle_noise(svc, connection);\n    }\n}\n```\n\n**Handler Processing:**\n**File:** `services/texture-service/src/texture_handler.c` (lines 132-194)\n\n```c\nTexture *texture_handler_create(TextureHandler *handler, const TextureInput *input,\n                                TextureError *err)\n{\n    // Validation\n    if (!_validate_texture_input(input, err)) return NULL;\n\n    // Build domain object\n    tx.id = _generate_uuid();\n\n    // Repository persistence\n    bool persist_ok = repository_save_texture(state->repo, &tx, err);\n\n    // Metrics update\n    if (state->metrics) metrics_counter_inc(state->metrics, \"texture_created_total\", 1);\n\n    return texture_clone(&tx, err);\n}\n```\n\n#### Step 5: Response Path\n\n1. Handler returns JSON response via `MHD_create_response_from_buffer()`\n2. Response flows back through libmicrohttpd\n3. Gateway receives HTTP response via libcurl\n4. Gateway parses JSON via `cJSON_Parse()`\n5. GraphQL resolver returns payload to client\n\n---\n\n## 3. Migration Strategy Proposal\n\n### 3.1 Overview: Phased Migration Approach\n\nThe migration will follow a **strangler fig pattern** with parallel systems operating simultaneously. This ensures zero downtime and allows for gradual rollout with easy rollback capability.\n\n```\nPhase 1: Infrastructure Setup (gRPC dependencies, proto definitions)\n    ↓\nPhase 2: Texture Service Dual-Protocol Support (gRPC + HTTP)\n    ↓\nPhase 3: API Gateway gRPC Client Integration (Feature Flag)\n    ↓\nPhase 4: Traffic Migration (Gradual rollout)\n    ↓\nPhase 5: Legacy HTTP Deprecation & Removal\n```\n\n### 3.2 Phase 1: Infrastructure Setup\n\n#### 3.2.1 Protocol Buffer Definition\n\n**New File:** `protos/texture.proto`\n\n```protobuf\nsyntax = \"proto3\";\n\npackage synestheticcanvas.texture.v1;\n\noption c_enable_arenas = true;\n\nservice TextureService {\n    rpc CreateTexture(CreateTextureRequest) returns (CreateTextureResponse);\n    rpc GetTexture(GetTextureRequest) returns (GetTextureResponse);\n    rpc ListTextures(ListTexturesRequest) returns (ListTexturesResponse);\n    rpc DeleteTexture(DeleteTextureRequest) returns (DeleteTextureResponse);\n    rpc GetTextureRaw(GetTextureRawRequest) returns (stream TextureChunk);\n}\n\nmessage CreateTextureRequest {\n    string name = 1;\n    string color_profile = 2;\n    uint32 width = 3;\n    uint32 height = 4;\n    float frequency = 5;\n}\n\nmessage CreateTextureResponse {\n    string id = 1;\n    uint32 width = 2;\n    uint32 height = 3;\n    string created_at = 4;\n}\n\nmessage GetTextureRequest {\n    string id = 1;\n}\n\nmessage GetTextureResponse {\n    string id = 1;\n    string name = 2;\n    string color_profile = 3;\n    uint32 width = 4;\n    uint32 height = 5;\n    string created_at = 6;\n}\n\nmessage ListTexturesRequest {\n    uint32 page_size = 1;\n    string page_token = 2;\n}\n\nmessage ListTexturesResponse {\n    repeated GetTextureResponse textures = 1;\n    string next_page_token = 2;\n    uint32 total_count = 3;\n}\n\nmessage DeleteTextureRequest {\n    string id = 1;\n}\n\nmessage DeleteTextureResponse {\n    bool success = 1;\n}\n\nmessage GetTextureRawRequest {\n    string id = 1;\n}\n\nmessage TextureChunk {\n    bytes data = 1;\n    uint64 offset = 2;\n    uint64 total_size = 3;\n}\n```\n\n#### 3.2.2 Root CMakeLists.txt Modifications\n\n**File:** `CMakeLists.txt` (Add after line 127)\n\n```cmake\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n#  gRPC and Protocol Buffers Support\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\noption(SC_ENABLE_GRPC \"Enable gRPC support for inter-service communication\" OFF)\n\nif(SC_ENABLE_GRPC)\n    find_package(Protobuf REQUIRED)\n    find_package(gRPC REQUIRED)\n\n    # Locate grpc_cpp_plugin for code generation\n    find_program(GRPC_CPP_PLUGIN grpc_cpp_plugin REQUIRED)\n\n    # Proto compilation function\n    function(add_proto_library TARGET_NAME PROTO_FILE)\n        get_filename_component(PROTO_PATH ${PROTO_FILE} DIRECTORY)\n        get_filename_component(PROTO_NAME ${PROTO_FILE} NAME_WE)\n\n        set(PROTO_SRCS \"${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/${PROTO_NAME}.pb.c\")\n        set(PROTO_HDRS \"${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/${PROTO_NAME}.pb.h\")\n        set(GRPC_SRCS \"${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/${PROTO_NAME}.grpc.pb.c\")\n        set(GRPC_HDRS \"${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/${PROTO_NAME}.grpc.pb.h\")\n\n        add_custom_command(\n            OUTPUT ${PROTO_SRCS} ${PROTO_HDRS} ${GRPC_SRCS} ${GRPC_HDRS}\n            COMMAND protobuf::protoc\n            ARGS --proto_path=${PROTO_PATH}\n                 --c_out=${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}\n                 --grpc_out=${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}\n                 --plugin=protoc-gen-grpc=${GRPC_CPP_PLUGIN}\n                 ${PROTO_FILE}\n            DEPENDS ${PROTO_FILE}\n        )\n\n        add_library(${TARGET_NAME} STATIC ${PROTO_SRCS} ${GRPC_SRCS})\n        target_include_directories(${TARGET_NAME} PUBLIC ${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR})\n        target_link_libraries(${TARGET_NAME} PUBLIC protobuf::libprotobuf-c gRPC::grpc)\n    endfunction()\n\n    # Compile proto files\n    add_proto_library(texture_proto ${PROJECT_SOURCE_DIR}/protos/texture.proto)\nendif()\n```\n\n### 3.3 Phase 2: Texture Service Dual-Protocol Support\n\nThe texture service will be modified to listen on **two ports simultaneously**: one for the existing HTTP/REST API and one for gRPC.\n\n#### 3.3.1 Texture Service CMakeLists.txt Modifications\n\n**File:** `services/texture-service/CMakeLists.txt` (Add after line 157)\n\n```cmake\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n#  gRPC Server Support (Optional)\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\noption(TEXTURESVC_ENABLE_GRPC \"Enable gRPC server endpoint\" OFF)\n\nif(TEXTURESVC_ENABLE_GRPC)\n    if(NOT TARGET texture_proto)\n        message(FATAL_ERROR \"texture_proto target not found. Build with -DSC_ENABLE_GRPC=ON\")\n    endif()\n\n    target_sources(texture_service PRIVATE\n        src/grpc_server.c\n        src/grpc_handlers.c\n    )\n\n    target_link_libraries(texture_service\n        PRIVATE\n            texture_proto\n            gRPC::grpc\n            gRPC::grpc++\n    )\n\n    target_compile_definitions(texture_service PRIVATE ENABLE_GRPC_SERVER=1)\nendif()\n```\n\n#### 3.3.2 Dual-Protocol Main Entry Point\n\n**New File:** `services/texture-service/src/grpc_server.c`\n\n```c\n/**\n * gRPC Server implementation for Texture Service\n * Runs alongside HTTP server for parallel protocol support\n */\n\n#include <grpc/grpc.h>\n#include <grpc/support/log.h>\n#include \"texture.grpc.pb-c.h\"\n#include \"texture_handler.h\"\n\n#define GRPC_DEFAULT_PORT 17002  // gRPC on separate port\n\ntypedef struct {\n    grpc_server *server;\n    grpc_completion_queue *cq;\n    TextureHandler *handler;\n    volatile bool running;\n} grpc_texture_server_t;\n\n// gRPC service method implementations\nstatic void handle_create_texture(grpc_texture_server_t *srv,\n                                  const CreateTextureRequest *req,\n                                  CreateTextureResponse *resp) {\n    TextureInput input = {\n        .name = req->name,\n        .color_profile = req->color_profile,\n        .width = req->width,\n        .height = req->height\n    };\n\n    TextureError err = {0};\n    Texture *tx = texture_handler_create(srv->handler, &input, &err);\n\n    if (tx) {\n        resp->id = strdup(tx->id);\n        resp->width = tx->width;\n        resp->height = tx->height;\n        // Format timestamp\n        char ts[32];\n        strftime(ts, sizeof(ts), \"%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ\", gmtime(&tx->created_at));\n        resp->created_at = strdup(ts);\n        texture_free(tx);\n    }\n}\n\ngrpc_texture_server_t *grpc_texture_server_create(TextureHandler *handler, uint16_t port);\nvoid grpc_texture_server_serve(grpc_texture_server_t *srv);\nvoid grpc_texture_server_stop(grpc_texture_server_t *srv);\nvoid grpc_texture_server_destroy(grpc_texture_server_t *srv);\n```\n\n#### 3.3.3 Modified main.c for Dual-Protocol\n\n**File:** `services/texture-service/src/main.c` (Modifications)\n\n```c\n#ifdef ENABLE_GRPC_SERVER\n#include \"grpc_server.h\"\n#include <pthread.h>\n\nstatic pthread_t g_grpc_thread;\nstatic grpc_texture_server_t *g_grpc_server = NULL;\n\nstatic void *grpc_server_thread(void *arg) {\n    grpc_texture_server_t *srv = (grpc_texture_server_t *)arg;\n    grpc_texture_server_serve(srv);\n    return NULL;\n}\n#endif\n\nint main(void)\n{\n    ServiceCtx svc;\n    config_set_defaults(&svc.cfg);\n\n    // ... existing HTTP setup ...\n\n#ifdef ENABLE_GRPC_SERVER\n    // Start gRPC server on separate port\n    uint16_t grpc_port = (uint16_t)sc_u16_from_env(\"TEXTURE_GRPC_PORT\", 17002);\n    g_grpc_server = grpc_texture_server_create(svc.handler, grpc_port);\n    if (g_grpc_server) {\n        pthread_create(&g_grpc_thread, NULL, grpc_server_thread, g_grpc_server);\n        log_msg(LOG_INFO, \"gRPC server started on port %u\", grpc_port);\n    }\n#endif\n\n    // Start HTTP daemon (existing code)\n    svc.daemon = MHD_start_daemon(...);\n\n    while (!g_shutdown)\n        sleep(1);\n\n#ifdef ENABLE_GRPC_SERVER\n    if (g_grpc_server) {\n        grpc_texture_server_stop(g_grpc_server);\n        pthread_join(g_grpc_thread, NULL);\n        grpc_texture_server_destroy(g_grpc_server);\n    }\n#endif\n\n    MHD_stop_daemon(svc.daemon);\n    return EXIT_SUCCESS;\n}\n```\n\n### 3.4 Phase 3: API Gateway gRPC Client Integration\n\n#### 3.4.1 Gateway CMakeLists.txt Modifications\n\n**File:** `api-gateway/CMakeLists.txt` (Add after line 147)\n\n```cmake\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n#  gRPC Client Support\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\noption(GATEWAY_ENABLE_GRPC \"Enable gRPC client for upstream services\" OFF)\n\nif(GATEWAY_ENABLE_GRPC)\n    if(NOT TARGET texture_proto)\n        message(FATAL_ERROR \"texture_proto target not found. Build with -DSC_ENABLE_GRPC=ON\")\n    endif()\n\n    target_sources(api_gateway PRIVATE\n        src/services/grpc_client.c\n    )\n\n    target_link_libraries(api_gateway\n        PRIVATE\n            texture_proto\n            gRPC::grpc\n    )\n\n    target_compile_definitions(api_gateway PRIVATE ENABLE_GRPC_CLIENT=1)\nendif()\n```\n\n#### 3.4.2 Feature Flag for Protocol Selection\n\n**File:** `api-gateway/src/services/service_client.c` (Modifications)\n\n```c\n// Add to sc_service_client_t structure\nstruct sc_service_client\n{\n    // ... existing fields ...\n\n#ifdef ENABLE_GRPC_CLIENT\n    /* gRPC channels */\n    grpc_channel *texture_grpc_channel;\n    bool use_grpc_texture;  // Feature flag\n#endif\n};\n\n// Modified initialization\nsc_service_client_t *sc_service_client_new(void)\n{\n    // ... existing code ...\n\n#ifdef ENABLE_GRPC_CLIENT\n    // Check feature flag\n    cli->use_grpc_texture = sc_bool_from_env(\"SC_USE_GRPC_TEXTURE\", false);\n\n    if (cli->use_grpc_texture) {\n        const char *grpc_addr = getenv(\"SC_TEXTURE_GRPC_URL\") ?: \"texture-service:17002\";\n        cli->texture_grpc_channel = grpc_insecure_channel_create(grpc_addr, NULL, NULL);\n        LOG_INF(\"gRPC enabled for texture service: %s\", grpc_addr);\n    }\n#endif\n\n    return cli;\n}\n\n// Modified call function\nint sc_service_client_call(sc_service_client_t *cli,\n                           sc_service_endp_t    endpoint,\n                           const char          *method,\n                           const char          *path,\n                           const char          *request_json,\n                           cJSON              **response_json,\n                           long                *http_status)\n{\n#ifdef ENABLE_GRPC_CLIENT\n    if (endpoint == SC_EP_TEXTURE && cli->use_grpc_texture) {\n        return sc_service_client_call_grpc(cli, path, request_json,\n                                           response_json, http_status);\n    }\n#endif\n\n    // Fall back to HTTP\n    return sc_service_client_call_http(cli, endpoint, method, path,\n                                       request_json, response_json, http_status);\n}\n```\n\n### 3.5 Phase 4: Traffic Migration Strategy\n\n#### 3.5.1 Deployment Configuration\n\n**Modified docker-compose.yml:**\n\n```yaml\ntexture_service:\n  environment:\n    <<: *default-env\n    SERVICE_PORT: 7002\n    TEXTURE_GRPC_PORT: 17002  # New gRPC port\n  ports:\n    - \"7002:7002\"    # HTTP (existing)\n    - \"17002:17002\"  # gRPC (new)\n\napi_gateway:\n  environment:\n    <<: *default-env\n    SC_USE_GRPC_TEXTURE: \"false\"  # Start with HTTP\n    SC_TEXTURE_GRPC_URL: \"texture_service:17002\"\n```\n\n#### 3.5.2 Gradual Rollout Process\n\n1. **Week 1:** Deploy texture service with dual-protocol support\n   - Monitor gRPC health endpoint\n   - Verify no performance degradation\n\n2. **Week 2:** Enable gRPC for internal testing\n   ```bash\n   export SC_USE_GRPC_TEXTURE=true  # On test gateway instances\n   ```\n\n3. **Week 3:** Canary deployment (5% traffic)\n   - Use load balancer weighted routing\n   - Monitor error rates and latency\n\n4. **Week 4:** Gradual increase (25% → 50% → 100%)\n\n5. **Week 5:** Deprecate HTTP endpoints (keep for rollback)\n\n### 3.6 Phase 5: Legacy HTTP Deprecation\n\nAfter successful migration:\n\n1. Remove HTTP endpoints from texture service\n2. Remove HTTP client code from gateway\n3. Update CMakeLists.txt to make gRPC the default\n4. Remove feature flags\n\n---\n\n## 4. Risk Assessment\n\n### 4.1 Risk 1: Build System Complexity\n\n**Severity:** HIGH\n**Likelihood:** HIGH\n\n**Description:**\nAdding gRPC to a pure C project introduces significant build system complexity. gRPC's C implementation requires:\n- Protocol Buffers compiler and runtime (`protobuf-c`)\n- gRPC C core library (`grpc`)\n- C++ toolchain (gRPC core has C++ dependencies)\n\n**Evidence:**\nCurrent CMakeLists.txt uses pure C with `-std=c17` standard (see `api-gateway/CMakeLists.txt`, line 66). gRPC-C requires linking against C++ runtime.\n\n**Mitigation:**\n- Use `grpc` C-core library instead of `grpc++`\n- Consider using `protobuf-c` for pure C protobuf support\n- Provide comprehensive Docker build images with pre-installed dependencies\n- Implement FetchContent fallbacks for missing system packages\n\n### 4.2 Risk 2: Performance Regression\n\n**Severity:** MEDIUM\n**Likelihood:** MEDIUM\n\n**Description:**\nThe current custom IPC uses UNIX domain sockets with minimal framing overhead (4-byte length prefix). gRPC introduces HTTP/2 framing overhead, protobuf serialization, and TLS handshakes (if enabled).\n\n**Evidence:**\nFrom `libs/sc_ipc/src/sc_ipc.c` (line 315-340), the current protocol uses simple `writev()` syscalls with minimal overhead.\n\n**Performance Characteristics Comparison:**\n\n| Aspect | Custom IPC | gRPC (HTTP/2) |\n|--------|-----------|---------------|\n| Framing overhead | 4 bytes | ~20-50 bytes |\n| Serialization | JSON/raw | Protobuf (binary) |\n| Connection setup | None (UNIX socket) | TCP + HTTP/2 handshake |\n| Latency (typical) | <1ms | 1-5ms |\n| Throughput | Higher | Lower (due to framing) |\n\n**Mitigation:**\n- Enable gRPC keep-alive to reduce connection overhead\n- Use streaming for large payloads (texture data)\n- Consider Unix Domain Socket transport for gRPC (if same host)\n- Benchmark before and after migration\n\n### 4.3 Risk 3: Operational Complexity During Transition\n\n**Severity:** MEDIUM\n**Likelihood:** HIGH\n\n**Description:**\nRunning a hybrid system with both HTTP and gRPC endpoints increases operational burden:\n- Doubled port exposure and monitoring\n- Complex debugging (two protocols)\n- Inconsistent behavior during transition\n\n**Evidence:**\nDocker-compose configuration shows services already expose multiple ports (see `docker-compose.yml`, lines 59-61).\n\n**Mitigation:**\n- Unified observability (OpenTelemetry supports both HTTP and gRPC)\n- Single health check endpoint covering both protocols\n- Clear runbooks for protocol-specific troubleshooting\n- Automated testing for both code paths\n\n### 4.4 Risk 4: Serialization Compatibility\n\n**Severity:** MEDIUM\n**Likelihood:** LOW\n\n**Description:**\nThe current system uses JSON for all payloads. Migration to Protobuf requires careful schema definition to maintain backward compatibility.\n\n**Evidence:**\nFrom `api-gateway/graphql/resolvers.c` (line 125), responses are parsed with `json_loads()`. From `services/texture-service/src/texture_handler.c` (line 373-394), JSON serialization is used throughout.\n\n**Mitigation:**\n- Define protobuf schemas that map 1:1 with existing JSON\n- Add `json_name` options for field name compatibility\n- Maintain JSON gateway for external consumers\n- Version protobuf schemas from the start\n\n### 4.5 Risk 5: Debugging and Observability\n\n**Severity:** LOW\n**Likelihood:** MEDIUM\n\n**Description:**\nJSON payloads are human-readable and easy to debug. Protobuf binary encoding requires additional tooling.\n\n**Evidence:**\nCurrent logging in `service_client.c` (lines 337-354) outputs JSON directly to stderr.\n\n**Mitigation:**\n- Use gRPC reflection service for introspection\n- Implement protobuf-to-JSON logging adapter\n- Use `grpcurl` or similar tools for debugging\n- Enable gRPC verbose logging during development\n\n---\n\n## 5. New gRPC-Based Communication Flow\n\n### 5.1 Request Flow Diagram\n\n```\n                              ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n                              │ Texture       │               │◄─┘\n                              │  │ (port 17002)  │     │ Handler       │               │\n                              │  └───────────────┘     └───────────────┘          │                    │\n  │─HTTP Request──►│                   │                    │\n  │                │                   │                    │\n  │                │──validate─────────│                    │\n  │                │                   │                    │\n  │                │──CreateTextureRequest (protobuf)──────►│\n  │                │                   │ Conclusion\n\nThe migration from custom IPC to gRPC is technically feasible and will provide significant benefits:\n\n**Benefits:**\n- Standardized interface definitions (Protocol Buffers)\n- Language-agnostic service contracts\n- Built-in streaming support for large payloads\n- Rich ecosystem (reflection, health checks, load balancing)\n- Better tooling for debugging and testing\n\n**Recommended Approach:**\n1. Start with the texture service as a pilot\n2. Use parallel protocol deployment (no big-bang cutover)\n3. Feature flag-controlled gradual rollout\n4. Maintain HTTP fallback for rollback capability\n\n**Timeline Estimate:**\n- Phase 1 (Infrastructure): 2-3 weeks\n- Phase 2 (Texture Service): 2-3 weeks\n- Phase 3 (Gateway Client): 2 weeks\n- Phase 4 (Migration): 4-6 weeks\n- Phase 5 (Cleanup): 1-2 weeks\n\n**Total:** 11-16 weeks for complete migration\n\n---\n\n## 7.
